- **Landscape right edge (Oct 1):** iOS reserves a safe area on both sides in landscape, but the camera is only on one side. On the right, the map controls sit 32px from the screen edge (not the full safe area), and the drone feed drags to 8px from it.
- **No resources pill (fire page, Oct 1, was "Add resources"):** an informational glass pill that says "No resources dispatched yet" in normal weight and in the red of the Crews tab dot (#E6352B). It has no outline; the frosted glass slightly darkens what is behind it, so it reads while the page scrolls under it. It floats 52px above the foot (40px higher than before), and a tap still opens the Crews tab.
- **Taps through a locked map band:** a tap on the fire page's map band reaches the control under it (full screen, legend chip, marker) even when iOS cancels the touch because the finger moved a hair: the tap's own click passes it through.
- **Large-title headers (Oct 1, every detail screen: candidate, fire, drone, station):** the top row holds back on the left and the header buttons (chat, notifications, profile) on the right. The title (title M, 26px) sits under them across the full width and wraps to a second line instead of being cut; the subtitle follows in normal text. Then 16px down to the qualifier band (40px, then 24px were tried: the header took too much of the screen). A drone's progress is an annotation under its place. On the station and drone screens the chat button opens the list of incident chats, as on the main screen.
- **Profile panel X (Oct 1):** the X appears exactly where the profile picture is and never moves. The panel's top row (Forest Fire Watch) is lined up with it, measured before the panel shows.
- **Blade titles (Oct 1):** 40px between a blade's or panel's title block (title and its subtitle) and the first element under it: incidents blade (title to switcher), area picker (to search), notifications (to the list), chats list, chat team and summary sheets, the candidates dropdown, card pickers.
- **Area table (Oct 1):** every row and the heading share one grid with fixed number columns (64px each, 16px apart), and every row has the same right padding, so Candidates and Fires line up in one column whatever row they are in (parent row with back, child rows with the forward arrow). Numbers and their headings stay right-aligned. "1 state", not "1 states".
- **Map never shows past the world (Oct 1):** the street map ends at 85° north and south. Zoom-out stops when the world exactly fills the map's height, and the map cannot be dragged past its top or bottom edge, so no empty band ever shows above or below it. Sideways the world repeats, as before.
- **Counts read in the singular for one:** 1 state, 1 county, 1 district.
- **Unconfirmed tag (Oct 1):** 1px dark grey inset outline (as the primary buttons, was 1.4px), no dropdown arrow, the text centred. It still opens Confirm / Dismiss and keeps its pulse.
- **Drone feed in the map band (Oct 1):** only the X, the Thermal / Visual switcher and maximize. The map button on the feed was removed: it competed with the video, and the X already returns to the map, from which full screen and the drone are one tap away.
- **Re-centre on full-screen maps (Oct 1, every full-screen map):** once the map has been moved or zoomed away from where it opened, a dark glass re-centre button (a target) appears just left of minimize; it glides back to the opening view (the station, pin or area in the centre) and disappears.
- **Station screen map band:** locked (no pan or zoom), at 400%, the station in the centre. A station opened without a map position (e.g. from a person's station card in a chat) is placed from its latitude and longitude.
- **Dispatch pill (fire page, Oct 1, was "No resources dispatched yet"):** "Please dispatch resources" with the stemmed forward arrow (→) after the words, in the Crews-dot red. 52px tall, as the round buttons; the glass is lighter: 50% instead of 62% and blur 10px instead of 16px.
- **Fire polygons (Oct 1):** burned area fill 20% more see-through (0.32), its outline 1px; held line 1px; burning edge 1px with a soft glow in its own orange on the outer side only (three widening, fading strokes clipped to outside the burned area), as if it were burning. Legend: burned area and edges drawn 1px; the burning edge icon has the same soft glow on one side.
- **Legend on full-screen maps (Oct 1):** the chips stack in a column at the top right, under the map's title: no sideways scrolling, nothing over the title. Small maps keep the scrolling row.
- **Dispatch pill stays (Oct 1):** "Please dispatch resources" shows on the Situation tab until orders are actually sent. A tap opens the Crews tab, where it is not shown; coming back to Situation (or reopening the fire) without sending, it is there again.
- **Card picker subtitle (Oct 1):** "Show or hide KPIs." then "Drag to reorder.", both in normal text.
- **Find a place watermark (Oct 1):** 17% grey (5% stronger). Only the eye blinks, a plain close and open every 3 s; the symbol itself never moves. It is centred in the free space between the note above and the keyboard (or the panel's bottom when the keyboard is down).
- **Blade titles in title M (Oct 1):** the incidents blade's title (the area) uses title M (26px semibold), as the titles of every other screen and panel.
- **Counts in bold (Oct 1):** in subtitles, the numbers are bold and the words regular: "**60** ignition candidates. **58** fires", "**58** counties". Apply to every similar count line.
- **Region selector (Oct 1):** under the header, the annotation "Select a region to explore." on its own line. The parent row's level-up control is the stemmed arrow pointing left (←), 48px like the rows' forward arrows (→): left goes up a level, right goes down. 16px between the column headings and the table.
- **Incidents list (Oct 1):** under the count line, the annotation "Choose an incident to explore.". Column headings in the region table's style (16px regular grey, no semibold). Likelihood and Status (was "State") sort with the region table's chevron (tap again to reverse). No chevrons at the end of the rows. A fire's description takes two lines: where it is, then what the tag does not already say (the containment % is on the tag, so it is not repeated).
- **Fire page sections (Oct 1):** Key figures, Weather and Terrain are separate groups: 56px between them (the section list's gap and its top margin, were 24px).
- **GPS card under the map (Oct 1):** 16px below the map band, the same as its distance to the screen's left and right edges, on the candidate and fire pages (was 32px and 12px).
- **Choose resources (Oct 1):** subtitle "Start by choosing a station."; the stations come first and Air support is the last option ("Helicopters, air tankers and drone swarms.").
- **Choose resources sheet (Oct 1):** no back button (Cancel and a tap outside close it); the title and subtitle line up with the list's text column (72px); subtitle "N available. Start by choosing a station.", wrapping instead of being cut.
- **Crews tab while choosing resources (Oct 1):** first element 56px under the tabs; tile titles keep two lines' room so their numbers line up ("0 of 4" level with "0 of 1" under a two-line title); Add resources 56px under the tiles; the chosen stations end 56px above Cancel and Send order, which stay fixed at the foot (Crews tab only).
- **Dialog titles (Oct 1, every dialog):** title M (26px semibold), as page titles; the item it concerns stays under it in normal text (e.g. "Confirm fire?" over "Santa Clarita"). The shared dialog (fit.js) and the screens' own dialogs.
- **Cards in sequence (Oct 1):** 8px between small cards, across and down (KPI grids, the GPS and time cards, stacked station cards, the stations chosen in Configure dispatch, the profiles on Login). A row of related cards is one group, never 56px apart; 56px only separates different groups.
- **Dispatch pill tint (Oct 1):** the glass carries a subtle red tint (a pale red at 62%) under the red words.
- **Kind band tag (Oct 1):** the tag inside the kind band ("Not contained") has no outline, only its tinted fill.
- **Header shadow over the map (Oct 1):** the header (title, band and tabs) is its own layer above the map, so its soft shadow falls on the map as on the page.
- **Crews tab cards (Oct 1):** Stations, Crews, Vehicles, People, Crews need relief and Tenders low on water are one card group built exactly like Key figures: three columns, title at the top (two lines kept, so every value sits at the same height), press and hold to reorder, the grey + tile to show or hide cards (list titled "Crews"). The group's "Estimate" note closes it, bottom right, at 60% opacity. Add resources sits 56px under the group.
- **Chat cards have no View button (Oct 1):** a card that points to its incident (ignition detected, ignition confirmed, topic cards) opens it when tapped; only real actions stay as buttons (e.g. "Projection and time in each stage").
- **One fire name everywhere (Oct 1):** a fire known only by its incident code (e.g. LAC-350855) is "Unnamed fire" on every screen: list, map card, fire page, chat (one shared rule, `__wfFireName`), so the storyline never switches names.
- **Map cards (Oct 1):** the big number (likelihood, time active, distance) is sized to leave 24px clear at each side of the card; the place or station name is 17px semibold. Every fire gets the full fire card (status tab, time active or "Start time not published.", name, place), also fires confirmed here and fires whose feed gives no details; never a bare "View".
- **Status tags are semibold (Oct 1):** 600, not bold, in lists, map cards, the kind band and unit rows. Their text colours meet WCAG AA (4.5:1) on their tint, also over the grey band: hot #963B00, green #186B2D, teal #00606A, amber #745200 (were #B84A00, #1E7A34, #00707A, #7A5600).
- **Find a place (Oct 1):** the field takes focus in the tap itself so the keyboard opens at once; the watermark settles once, centred between the field and the keyboard as it opens, and then stays put (it no longer follows the message or the keyboard closing).
- **Night detections (Oct 1):** an ignition candidate detected with the sun below the horizon (solar altitude from the detection's own time and place, real data) carries a small black moon (a slim crescent, less than half, tilted about 25°) in the centre of its map circle, over the circle's fill, with no border (a border doesn't help reading), right after its name in the incidents list, inside its kind band icon (centred over the circle's fill, no border, as on the map; also on the detail page's map), and a "Night detection" line on its map card.
- **Incidents blade (Oct 1):** the title and "Choose an incident to explore." only; the counts sit on the switcher (All, Candidates, Fires), in bold after each label.
- **Map controls (Oct 1, every map):** one order everywhere: Find a place (only on the main screen's map, where no place is chosen yet), Re-centre, then Send drone (where offered) and full screen. On a map in the page they stack in a column at the bottom right; on full screen they line up in a row along the bottom. Re-centre is on every map that can be moved (not on locked bands) and is dimmed and inactive while the map is still centred where it opened.
- **Full-screen map title (Oct 1):** the main map on full screen (and in landscape) carries the place in view as its title and the level above as its description (Los Angeles / California; California / United States; a country has none), in the title box used by the fire map.
- **Switchers with counts (Oct 1, incidents switcher):** options are sized by their labels plus an equal share of the spare room, so every option, chosen or not, has the same padding around its text; the track's side padding is halved (6px) to make room; the yellow takes the chosen option's measured box and glides (position and width). Each label reserves its semibold width, so choosing an option never shifts the text.

- **No maximize / minimize on maps in landscape (Oct 1):** a phone turned sideways shows the map full screen with no full-screen button; turning the phone upright is the way back. Applies to every map.
- **Dialogs (Oct 1, every bottom dialog: KPI pickers, topic picker, people sheet, choose resources):** no back button at the title; the title moves left into its place, lined up with the list text. Each dialog has a grabber like the other blades (72px line 8px from the top edge, 40px clear above the content): a tap on it closes the dialog, and a swipe down closes it (on the grabber always; anywhere when the list doesn't scroll). A tap outside still closes it.
- **Tabs (Oct 1):** 16px between the tabs and what follows (and 16px between the map and the next element); the grey track is 12px, 4px taller than the 8px lime indicator; the indicator fills its tab with the same 2px inset on every side (top, bottom and the track's edges), centred in the tab, gliding between tabs.
- **Night ignitions on a fire (Oct 1):** the start time reads in the fire's own local time with AM / PM (the zone added when it differs from the phone's, e.g. "Started 3:46 AM PDT"), followed by the moon when the sun was down there and then. Under the time card, the subtle button "Report night ignition" opens a chat with an officer of the local force (county sheriff in the US, GNR SEPNA in Portugal), with the fire's card (place, start time, GPS) and "Reporting a night ignition, can you please investigate?" already sent; the officer answers and the chat goes on in character (Claude role play when a key is set).
- **Crews KPIs (Oct 1):** five more cards in the Crews picker: Relief due within 3 h, Fresh crews, Longest shift, Water in tanks, Aircraft dropping (from the same scripted crew figures as the others).
- **Night moon persists (Oct 1):** a night candidate keeps its moon, centred in the circle with no border, on every map where it appears, including its own detail map when selected and explored.
- **Buttons (Oct 1):** primary lime has no dark outline; secondary (Cancel, Dismiss and the like) moves from black with lime text to a reflective mid grey with white text, everywhere (shared dialog, chat actions, sheets).
- **Big numbers on map cards (Oct 1):** at most 45px (30% below the old 64px), whatever room the card has; they still shrink to fit when the number is long.
- **Choose resources (Oct 1):** each station in its own white card (16px radius, 8px apart); the title lines up with the cards' left edge, 40px above the first element, and wraps rather than being cut; step 2 lists the resources with a full-width line between rows and 36px icons (as on the Crews tab); Cancel and Apply stay fixed at the foot while the list scrolls; tall sheets stop 48px below the visible top of the screen.
- **Fire full-screen map header (Oct 1):** a full-width container (16px from the sides, 16px padding top and bottom) with two groups: the place (title) over its county, then the status band (dot, "Active fire", the stage tag). Upright they stack; in landscape they share one row, the place anchored left and the status right. The legend sits 32px below it. The plain full-screen title (main map) gets the same 16px padding.
- **Full-screen legends (Oct 1, every map, upright and landscape):** a column anchored on the left, under the title, so the chips' circles line up.
- **Landscape right edge (Oct 1):** on every map, legends and controls sit 32px from the right edge (the camera side keeps its safe area).
- **Re-centre (Oct 1):** dimmed and inactive until the person has moved the map themselves (pan, flick or pinch); re-centring dims it again.
- **Night candidate (Oct 1):** "Detected" reads in the candidate's local time with AM / PM, followed by the moon; the candidate's own map pin carries the moon too. Report night ignition appears only once it is a fire.
- **Consistency pass (Oct 1):** time units always spaced ("16 h ago", "2 d ago"), "Just now" under a minute; the Unconfirmed pill has no outline; a qualifier band's kind wraps to two lines rather than being cut; every row of KPI cards shares one number size (the smallest any card needs); estimate notices use the 13px note style (the drone's "Estimate", the vertical card labels); count badges are the lime 24px badge everywhere (station screen included), with 14px regular numbers (Oct 7; 16px semibold before); white cards share a 16px radius; chat list rows have no chevron and no "Chat" prefix; the map credit keeps 16px from the edge.
- **Spacing pass (Oct 1):** section labels ("Key figures", "Language", group labels, the chat note) line up with the cards at 16px; chat bubbles have 16px padding and sit 8px apart; the chat header puts its title on its own line under the buttons, wrapping to two lines; a quick reply already sent is not offered again.
- **Night ignition report (Oct 1):** the report chat is titled "Night ignition report" over the fire's place, with one member besides you: the local police captain (photo: Arthur Ogleznev on Unsplash, Unsplash License). The captain investigates: asks how it was detected, people or vehicles seen, lightning, power lines and roads, keeping the origin intact, photos; never talks about fighting the fire. Quick replies match (Detected by satellite, No one seen nearby, A vehicle was seen nearby, No lightning last night, The origin is preserved, Could it be arson?). Once reported, the fire page shows "✓ Night ignition reported" (a tap opens the chat) instead of the button.
- **Suggestion chips (Oct 1):** lime background at 70% opacity (was 80%).
- **Resolved fires (Oct 1):** no "Simulate this fire" button; a resolved fire's chat is read-only history.
- **Titled groups (Oct 1, every similar screen):** a group of cards under a title (the resolution summary's "Crews" (Oct 9: renamed, no counts line under it), a profile's achievements) has its title in title M, then 24px to the cards, and 24px between the cards.
- **Forces cards (Oct 1):** a full-width line between a station's header and its people; aircraft get the helicopter badge (a shield in the station-crest look, shared from prefs.js) beside their name, like the station crests.
- **Chats blade (Oct 1):** the chat button (main screen, and the station and drone screens) opens the area's chats as a blade, the same as the notifications and the profile: title and subtitle, the Open / Resolved switcher fixed under them, the chats as white cards 8px apart, scrolling 56px under the switcher, the simulation note 56px under the last card; X, a tap outside or the grabber closes it. Opening a chat goes to that chat.
- **Notifications (Oct 1):** each update is a white card (16px radius, 8px apart), 40px under the title, no chevron (as the chat cards).
- **Map cards (Oct 1):** 60% more room between a KPI's title and its number (Likelihood, Distance); the station crest on a station card is 1.8 times bigger (54% of the card width).
- **Label to control (Oct 1):** every group label sits 8px above what it names and lines up at 16px, whether the control is a switcher or a white drawer card (Chat settings, Connected data sources).
- **Disabled buttons (Oct 1, every text button):** a disabled button takes the secondary formula, faded: grey at 50% with its white label at 90% (dark theme: its own faded grey), no lime, not tappable. Example: Create invite link before a key is saved.
- **Text and actions (Oct 1, app-wide):** 24px between text and the field or button that follows (and button to the text after it), 24px between stacked actions of one group (Replace key, then Remove); side-by-side pairs keep 16px. Labels keep their own 8px to what they name.
- **Notification icons (Oct 1):** each notification card leads with its type in a 40px round: the ignition candidate's marker (new detection, likelihood changes), the fire's stage icon in the stage's colours (as in the chat timeline: Surveillance an eye, Concluding a drop, First alert a bell...), the station's house for station updates. Titles wrap to two lines rather than being cut.
- **Status tags in bands (Oct 1, every band: candidate, fire, station, drone):** the tag sits 4px from the band's edges (44px tall in the 52px band), so it nearly fills the band's end.
- **Station page (Oct 1):** the crest sits left of the title in the header; under the map, the GPS card (coordinates, then Directions as an up-and-right arrow 16px left of the copy icon) and right below it the fires within 60 km in a white card (8px apart); Command and Resources in white cards; an empty resource ("No aircraft based at this station.") centred in its card; no "Station" section (crest, name, type and location are already above), only a Details card when the station has other details; no foot button (Directions moved into the GPS card).
- **Station map card (Oct 1):** half the space above and below the crest.
- **Dispatch orders sent (Oct 1):** no check mark on the title (each station card has its own, now 36px); the unit counts sit centred under that check mark; Reconfigure (secondary, left) and Done (primary, right) share one row.
- **Crews tab (Oct 1):** KPI cards default to Crews, Vehicles and People; the others (relief, water, longest shift…) are off until switched on in the picker; no Stations card. Station cards: a full-width line between the station and its units; the vertical level bars next to unit tags are gone.
- **Fire stage icons (Oct 1):** in "Time in each stage" (fire card blade and resolution summary) each stage icon is a 56px round with a 32px glyph, balanced with the title and annotation beside it (16px apart).
- **Spread projection screen (Oct 1, supersedes the bottom-panel layout):** fixed header; under it one scrolling page: the Now / +1 h / +3 h / +6 h switcher (stays at the top while scrolling, 16px around), a 600px map band (twice the old window; locked, full-screen button), then the KPI cards, the note and View fire as a secondary button. The map shows the current outline (dark grey) and, at +1 / +3 / +6 h, the projected shape in the lime at 50% (dashed dark edge): the current outline grown about its centre to 1.3× / 1.5× / 2× its area. Without a model run the outline is illustrative. Projection cards always show numbers, each marked Estimate: area at the horizon (+ growth), burning edge % (+ its length), held by (time, or "6+ h · Not in 6 h"). The note explains the lime ("where the fire can be in N h, X% more area than now").
- **Spread projection header (Oct 1, later):** the large-title layout of the other detail screens (back on the top row, the title and its region under it); the Now / +1 h / +3 h / +6 h switcher sits in the header in place of the kind band, so it is always visible; no View fire button (Back returns to the fire's chat).
- **One fire page (Oct 1):** every fire opens as the active fire page (header with the state tag, Situation / Crews tabs). The old "Dispatch orders sent" blade with Done is not used; a link that lost the fire's "Confirmed from" note (projection, chat) or a candidate that has left the feed still opens the active fire page, timed from the confirmation.
- **Fire stage chip and stage history (Oct 1):** in every header band the tag sits 6px from the band's edge (40px tall, 20px radius; was 4px / 44px). On a fire the chip leads with the time in the current stage, one unit only ("2 d", "5 h", "40 min"), before the stage name. A tap on the band slides the earlier stages out from under the chip, one at a time to the left (oldest on the left, each tucked under the next, only its left corners rounded, each in its stage colour and icon); the kind ("Active fire") folds away; a second tap stacks them back. Fitting: the current stage keeps its name; earlier chips show icon, time and name, then names cropped evenly, then icon and time, then the icon alone. Times come from the fire's chat when it has one, else they are estimated from typical stage durations since the start (the aria label says so). Only earlier stages are shown, never later ones.
- **Notification icons by topic (Oct 1, later):** a station update shows what it is about: a vehicle returning to base → truck; crews released / station available → crew; an aircraft → helicopter; the station's house only when none fits.
- **Blade type icon:** the notifications and chats blades show their type icon (bell, chat bubble; 28px, dark grey, not a button) before the area title, as the station crest before a station's name.
- **Equal-width tags:** status tags in a list share one width (the widest in the list, measured after translation, label centred) via `data-wf-eqw` (fit.js); the fires list in the incidents blade uses it.
- **Blade swipe on empty spaces:** on a blade whose content scrolls, the rows scroll and never move the blade; the grabber and the blade's empty spaces (header, footer, around and below the rows) swipe it open or closed. All blades.
- **Stage chip, vertical history (Oct 1, supersedes the sideways stack):** the chip reads icon, stage name (semibold), then the time in regular weight, right-aligned ("Not contained  for 10 h"). A tap on the band drops the earlier stages down under it, one at a time, newest first (going down goes back in time): every chip the same width, each tucked 14px under the one above (only its bottom corners show rounded), in its stage colour and icon, its time ("2 h") right-aligned so durations read as a column. A second tap folds them back up. The stack floats over the tabs and map.
- **Projection on the fire page (Oct 1):** on every fire's Situation tab, first under the map (where the GPS card was), the projection switcher Now / +1 h / +3 h / +6 h, Now by default; the map shows the current outline and, at +1 / +3 / +6 h, the outline grown 1.3× / 1.5× / 2× in area in lime at 50%. 56px under it, the GPS / time / report group.
- **Station map tooltip:** the crest sits half as far from the state tab above it and from the station name below it as before.
- **Resource lists as cards (Oct 1):** a station's vehicles, crews and aircraft are each their own white card (16px corners), 8px apart, not rows in one container. Same for people in a crew or command-team dialog.
- **Chat in people dialogs:** no X (the grabber closes it) and no foot "Chat with …" button: the chat is a round primary (lime) button with the chat icon, inside the crew chief's card; for the command team (a group chat) it sits at the title's right, where the X was. Unread count as a badge on it.
- **Resolving is light blue (Oct 1):** the Resolving stage (ANEPC "Em resolução") uses a positive light blue everywhere (stage icon #1F64A6 on #E3EEFB; status tag #185A93 on 14% blue), not amber. Amber stays for partial US containment.
- **Stage chips:** no outlines and no drop shadows; overlapping chips separate by their colours.
- **Crews on a held or resolved fire (Oct 1):** the Crews tab still lists the units that fought the fire, station by station (same cards), but read-only: no KPI mini cards, no add / reconfigure, no live status tags (fatigue, water, drops) or level bars; each unit says what it is (e.g. "VFCI. 5 firefighters.", "10,000 L."); each station says how many units it had on the fire.
- **Stage colour spectrum (Oct 1, app-wide):** First alert yellow (#6B5200 on #FFF1A0, Oct 5: a true yellow, apart from the lime of ignition candidates), Ongoing yellow-orange (#8A4B00 on #FDE7C4), Crews on scene red-orange (#B3261E on #FBE1DC), Resolving blue (#1F64A6 on #E3EEFB), Concluding light green (#2F6B12 on #E2F2D2), Surveillance dark green (#14532D on #CFE5D6), Closed grey (#48484A on #E0E0E5); all ≥5:1 text contrast. Feed tones follow (watch = dark green).
- **Stage stack, both ways:** open, the earlier stages drop below the current chip and up to four stages still to come rise above it in greys (nearest darkest #C7C7CC/#2C2C2E, then #D1D1D6, #DCDCE0, #E8E8EC; no times). The current chip grows 8px each side and gets a 2px ring in its text colour; each chip away from it is 2px narrower each side than the one before (centred), so the current one reads as the parent. Upcoming chips tuck under the one below them (top corners rounded), earlier ones under the one above (bottom corners rounded).
- **Projection shapes (Oct 1, later; supersedes the uniform growth):** +1 h / +3 h / +6 h grow the burned area 1.6× / 2× / 3.6× (fire page and Projection screen). The projected outline is not a scaled copy: it grows unevenly, most downwind, with its own wobble at each horizon (seeded per fire, stable), never inside the current outline; its edge is a dashed burning edge (orange), fill lime at 50%.
- **Where the projection shows:** only on fires not yet held (up to Resolving); from Concluding on, and on held fires, the switcher is hidden and what was under it moves up. Full screen: the switcher sits bottom-left, level with the re-centre and minimise buttons on the right. The fire page's map band moves with the finger: a vertical swipe on it scrolls the page (never the map), a sideways drag pans the map and a pinch zooms it; a tap reaches what is under it (full screen, legend, marker). Touches never pass through the page to the map, which broke scrolling and sent taps to the wrong card (Oct 1).
- **Stage stack, simplified (Oct 1, supersedes the tapered widths):** the current chip keeps one size open or closed (no growth); every other chip, above or below, is the same width, 8px narrower than it (4px each side), centred. The current chip's ring when open is its text colour at 50%. Upcoming greys step further apart: #B4B4BA (text #1C1C1E), #D1D1D6, #E0E0E5, #EBEBEF.
- **Stage stack, separate chips (Oct 1, latest):** open, the chips no longer tuck under each other: each is fully rounded like the current chip, 4px apart, sliding up and down. Above, only the next two stages, light (#DCDCE0 with #2C2C2E text, then #EEEEF1 with #545458), their time reading "TBD"; below, every earlier stage, in the stage colours.
- **Stage tag is one shared component (Oct 1):** `window.__wfStageTag(fire)` (chat.js) gives a fire's stage, label, colours and icon from the stage spectrum; the incidents list tags (with the stage icon, equal widths), the fire band, the map tooltip, the full-screen map header and the Projection header all use it.
- **Lists as cards:** incidents (candidates and fires) and locations (countries, regions, districts) are white cards (16px corners), 8px apart, no dividers; the selected location has a 2px dark ring.
- **Shadows:** every drop shadow in the app has 0, 0 offsets (blur only), legends included. Stage chips have no shadow (their colours separate them; removed Oct 1); upcoming greys #DADADF and #E4E4E8. The open stage stack closes on a tap on the current stage or anywhere outside it.
- **Stack folding back** is animated like unfolding: the chips glide home one at a time (same timing), then fade.
- **Fire map on the page is live:** touches on the map band reach the map (pan, zoom, its controls); the rest of the page scrolls as before.
- **Demo profiles (Oct 1):** Susana Vasconcellos removed; the administrator is James Whitmore (JW, English, us-m1 photo), not a real person's name or photo.

- **Demo profile list (Oct 1):** no snapping; it scrolls freely with the phone's own momentum, and the list ends with a normal 32px margin.
- **Invite link (Oct 1):** no Copy button: Share is full width; the grey link box shows the whole link plus one row holding the copy icon on the right, and a tap anywhere on the box copies it. The dark tooltip (as on chat people) says "Copied to clipboard" above the icon and the icon turns to a check for 1.8 s.
- **Confirm fire? dialog (Oct 1):** a full-width primary "Chat with the team first" sits above Dismiss fire | Confirm fire (24px apart) and opens the candidate's team chat, so the team can talk before deciding.
- **Ignition candidate stage (Oct 1):** the stage spectrum starts on the lightest lime (#F7FCDC, a shade lighter than First alert) with dark grey text and the outline circle, not grey; it applies wherever the stage tag shows.
- **Fire map band (Oct 1):** a tap that moves a hair still reaches the map's buttons and legend; a new touch forgets fingers left over from a pinch, so the map keeps answering after a zoom.
- **Profile photo picker (Oct 1):** the "Or pick one" label is centred on the screen, like the photo and Change photo above it.
- **Create profile (Oct 1):** styled as the grey Add resources button: the same grey (solid #E3E3E8, the 12% grey over the page) and corners, a bare grey plus where the photos sit (no circle); its title and description keep their text styles.
- **Small maps are locked (Oct 1, every minimised map: fire page, ignition candidate, station, projection):** no pan and no zoom; a vertical swipe on them scrolls the page; taps still reach the legend chips and the map's buttons (full screen, drone, re-centre). Full screen pans and zooms as before.
- **Fire map framing (Oct 1):** the small map frames the current outline and, while the fire is not held, its whole +6 h projected shape, zooming out as much as needed so every horizon (Now, +1 h, +3 h, +6 h) stays fully on screen. The projected shape is one shared definition (fit.js `__wfGrowXY`) used by the map and the framing.
- **Stage tag:** no shadow on the current stage when the stack is open either.
- **Secondary button (Oct 1, every one in the app):** grey 10% lighter, #737376 with white text (4.7:1); at night #6A6A6E with #F2F2F7; the disabled formula follows the same grey, faded. Sign out is a secondary button.
- **Orders sent (Oct 1):** the round back sits above the title as on other screens; the primary is "Return" ("Voltar") instead of Done, and both go back to the page you came from (the fire page, the chat, or the map).
- **Shared elements inherit (standing rule, Oct 1):** a change to a common element (a button type, tag, card, dialog) applies to every sibling and related use across the app, now and in future work.
- **Crews tab stations (Oct 1):** folded by default; unfold one with its chevron; leaving the page and coming back folds them all again.
- **Stage stack in full screen (Oct 1):** the full-screen map header shows the same stage tag as the page (stage and time in it); a tap unfolds the stack over the map, a tap outside or on the tag folds it. The stage stack exists only on the fire page and its full-screen map.
- **Scroll on return (Oct 1, app-wide):** every screen and every panel (dialogs, sheets, Create profile, Choose a demo profile) opens at its top when you come back to it; the one exception is coming back with a back control, which returns you to the place you left (remembered even if the screen reloads).
- **Actions tied to an item (Oct 1, standing):** buttons that act on one specific item sit 24px below it (as Remove | Replace key under the key field): Edit profile's Remove | Save sit 24px under the profile card; groups stay 56px apart.
- **Full-screen map card (Oct 1):** under a state or country it reads how many subdivisions the area has ("58 counties", "20 districts", "27 states"); under a county, its state. The country name is gone.
- **Legend counts (Oct 1):** each marker chip on the main map shows its count in the area after the label, semibold dark grey (Ignition candidates 9, Active fires, Resolved fires, Fire stations).
- **Round button press, dark ones too (Oct 1):** every round button, dark glass ones included (map controls, drone feed), presses exactly like the chat and notifications buttons at the top: swell, 2px dark grey outline pulsing to 150% and fading, quarter turn on X and +. No lime pulse anywhere.
- **Search boxes (Oct 1):** full width of their row again (the 20%-narrower, centred version was tried and dropped).
- **Selected list item (Oct 1):** a 1px dark grey outline (was 2px), in every list (area picker, chosen stations).
- **Station tooltip tag (Oct 1):** the tag at the top of a station tooltip ("1 fire", "Available") is always at least as wide as "Available", so busy and free stations show the same tag width.
- **Dark round buttons, press (Oct 1, later; refines the round button press above):** no edge line of their own (their dark glass is already the pulse colour); on press they swell like the chat button at the top and pulse a 1px dark grey outline (light buttons keep 2px), and they do not dim while held.
- **Unit rows on the Crews tab (Oct 1):** the status tag sits on the first row, beside the unit name, anchored to the right of the card; the description runs under both.
- **Area picker (Oct 1):** no "Select a region to explore." sentence under the title; the search box says it instead ("Select a region to explore") at every level.
- **Report night ignition (Oct 1, experiment):** no longer a full-width subtle button under the time card; it is a tag inside the time card, just before the clock: the Resolving tag background (#E3EEFB), dark blue text (#1F64A6), the start time's text style (16px regular).
- **Stage stack, upcoming stages (Oct 1):** the grey pills of stages still to come read "Next" (annotation style: 16px regular, mid grey) instead of "TBD".
- **Sign out refreshes the app (Oct 1):** Sign out in the user settings goes through the reset page (reset.html?out=1): it clears the cached copies of the app and loads the newest version, then opens the sign-in screen. Saved settings and profiles stay; only the sign-in is cleared. The plain reset link still clears everything.
- **Dark round buttons, pulse (Oct 1, fix):** the 1px dark grey line starts at the swollen edge of the button (not under it, where dark on dark hid it), so it shows from the first moment, and still ends 50% beyond the button.
- **Find a place opens fully, every time (Oct 1, fix):** on iPhone the keyboard opening during the unfold could leave the panel painted folded (only the top band). After the unfold the open edge is painted again, so the panel always covers the map down to its bottom edge.
- **Round buttons, one shape (Oct 1):** every round button is a true circle: the light ones at the top (chat, notifications, close X, back) no longer take the 20px corner of the .opt rule, so they match the dark map buttons. No lime pulse exists anywhere (the option was removed from the shared press code).
- **Main map default layers (Oct 1):** the main map opens with only the ignition candidates showing; Active fires, Resolved fires and Fire stations start hidden and their legend chips show them (each remembered for the session, on the main map only; other maps keep their own settings).
- **Brazil live (Oct 1):** Brazil (27 states) and its Amazônia Legal area now show live data (NASA FIRMS candidates, INPE fire events) like Portugal and California, in the area picker, maps, lists and Find a place.
- **Login (Oct 1):** 16px under "Log in to your account": "Alpha version" in the label style (16px regular) in the hi-vis yellow, then "2 Oct 2026" in normal text (was "Sep 2026") (17px regular, white).
- **Drone icon (Oct 1):** a drone seen from the front (two propellers, arms, body, legs and camera) replaces the top view, which read like an expand/command symbol next to full screen. Same icon on the Send drone button and on Drone verification.
- **Ignition candidate projection (Oct 1, experiment):** under the candidate's map, the same Now / +1 h / +3 h / +6 h switcher as on a fire. Now shows the marker only; +1 h and on draw the shape the ignition would burn if it is a fire (lime at 50%, dashed orange edge), grown from the marker hour by hour with the forecast there (Open-Meteo: wind speed and direction push the head downwind, temperature speeds it up; the back creeps upwind, the flanks widen). The map zooms out just enough to show the whole shape; the marker stays on top.
- **Full-screen map buttons (Oct 1, fix):** the page moved out of the way under a full-screen map takes no taps at all (its map layers were catching the taps meant for the full-screen buttons).
- **Dark round buttons, pulse (Oct 1, later):** the pulse line is 2px, like the light buttons (it still starts at the swollen edge).
- **Sign out screen (Oct 1):** plain black while the app refreshes: no logo, no words.
- **Loading screen after log in (Oct 1):** the colour wave stays as it was; the black-and-white photo ahead of it starts 20% darker and brightens with the counter to its normal lightness at 100%.
- **Switchers, even padding (Oct 1):** every segmented switcher keeps the same 10px between the yellow and the track on all four sides; options lose their own side padding to make room (incidents list switcher, projection switcher on the full-screen fire map).
- **Grabber to text (Oct 1):** on the main screen the top blade's subtitle sits 21px above its grabber, the same as the bottom blade's first line below its grabber (was 36px); every grabber sits 8px from its blade's edge (checked on both blades).
- **Full-screen area map (Oct 1):** the area card (e.g. Portugal, 20 districts) sits at the top right; the legend column starts at the top left on the same line.
- **More data sources (Oct 1):** satellite candidates now also come from NASA FIRMS MODIS (Terra, Aqua) and Landsat (30 m, US and Canada), and from NOAA GOES-18/19 geostationary scans every 10 minutes over the Americas (the last hour; a GOES-only spot needs two scans). Listed in Preferences › data sources with NOAA GOES and Open-Meteo.
- **Candidate projection uses terrain (Oct 1):** Copernicus elevation (through Open-Meteo) around the marker; each direction reaches further uphill and less downhill.
- **Chat keeps its place:** every chat opens where you left it (remembered per chat on the phone), the one exception to "opens at its top"; left at the end, it opens at the end and follows new messages. Sending a message goes to the end.
- **Team of this fire:** each member is its own white card (16px corners), 8px apart, no dividers.
- **Stations in a fire's chat and its dispatch agree:** the dispatch offers exactly the stations whose coordinators are in the incident chat (with crews free, since they said they are available), further stations only after them when reinforcing. Never fewer than two stations and two station chiefs in a chat.
- **Candidate projection on the full-screen map (Oct 1):** the Now / +1 h / +3 h / +6 h switcher also sits on the full-screen candidate map, in the dark map-control style (dark glass, white options, the yellow on the chosen one), on the buttons' centre line, 8px left of them; the two switchers share one choice.
- **Fire time card (Oct 1, later):** no "9 d 22 h active" line (the stage tag already says how long); the card holds only when it started, in normal text. Report night ignition is a tag glued to the card's top edge, centred, with flat top corners like the tags on the map cards (Resolving blue, annotation text); the card makes room for it.
- **More open sources (Oct 1, later):** CAL FIRE incidents add the California fires NIFC lacks (a CAL FIRE fire within ~4 km of a NIFC fire counts as the same fire); EFFIS (Copernicus) burnt areas give Portuguese fires their mapped shape (last 30 days, nearest within 3 km); the weather row on zoomed-in maps adds the official fire danger in Portugal (IPMA, nearest municipality, today) and the air quality (US AQI in the Americas, European AQI elsewhere). All listed in Preferences › data sources.
- **Spacing scale (Oct 1, standing):** distances between objects (gaps and margins) use only 8, 16, 24, 32, 48 and 56px; every other value moved to the nearest one, ties to the smaller (10 and 12 → 8; 14, 18 and 20 → 16; 22 → 24; 38 and 40 → 32). Values under 8px inside one component (a title and its description, an icon and its label) stay as they are; paddings are unchanged.
- **Data sources list (Oct 2):** each source has a short description of what it brings to the app, in the annotation style, across the full row (under the switch too), 8px under the row's dates; Portuguese included.
- **Switchers and round buttons over maps (Oct 2, standing):** a segmented switcher sitting on a map uses the dark map-control style (black 58% glass, white options, the yellow on the chosen one), like the round map buttons. The Projection screen's full-screen map now has the Now / +1 h / +3 h / +6 h switcher too (on the buttons' line, 8px left of them), and the fire's full-screen switcher turned dark. The re-centre button uses the same dark formula; while the map is centred only its icon dims.
- **Projection screen (Oct 2):** the chats, notifications and profile buttons sit at the top right like on every incident screen; the map band is sized to the screen (screen height minus the header and the KPI row, at least 280px), so the KPI cards show under it without scrolling.
- **Projection map legend (Oct 2):** the map shows its legend chips: Burned area with its area and perimeter now (e.g. "30 acres. 1 mi") and, from +1 h, "In 6 h" (lime, orange edge) with the projected area and perimeter; each chip shows or hides its shape. Same on the full-screen map.
- **Chat stage cards (Oct 2):** the stage and time line sits on the icon's centre line (a 32px row), the line under it keeps its 8px. The card's action and the sheet it opens read "Progress and projection" (was "Projection and time in each stage").
- **Stage stacks (Oct 2):** every stack shows only the next stage above the current one (not two). On the full-screen fire map the header uses the page header's sizes (26px title, 17px subtitle, the 52px status band with its 40px tag), so the stage tag and its open stack sit and space exactly as on the page.
- **Configuring dispatch from the chat (Oct 2):** opens on the Crews tab with the configuration; the Situation tab shows the fire (map, figures) and Crews returns to the configuration, the draft kept.
- **Add resources (Oct 2):** a white card with the + in a 56px grey square like the mini KPI + tiles, the label on its right.
- **AI suggested pack (Oct 2):** while nothing is chosen, a card above Add resources (lime square with a sparkle): one tap fills the plan with the two nearest available stations (each with its suggested order, the first leading) and air support; every card can still be changed or removed.
- **Suggestion chips (Oct 2, standing):** white at 70% like your own messages (was the lime at 70%). "Chat with the team first" in the Confirm fire dialog is such a chip, with the chat icon on its left, no longer a full-width primary button.
- **Re-centre on full-screen maps (Oct 2):** a map band that is locked on the page (the ignition candidate's) still gets the re-centre button once full screen, where it pans; the projection switcher makes room for it, 8px to its left.
- **Full-screen map controls (Oct 2, standing):** the round controls stack vertically on the right (re-centre, drone, full screen; search on top where it exists), 16px from the right; a projection switcher takes the width to their left, 8px away, level with the lowest button.
- **One round-button size (Oct 2, standing):** every round button is 44px (radius 22), light and dark; the drone feed's close, maximize and back buttons moved from 52px to 44px.
- **Fixes (Oct 2):** the area picker's search took no typing (a code comment had swallowed its value; fixed). Full-screen projection switchers (candidate, Projection) sit at a fixed place, left of the stacked controls (bottom 20px, right 68px), so they never vanish while the map redraws.
- **Candidate projection zoom (Oct 2):** the map band keeps its zoom locked at every horizon; the projected shape simply fills it as it grows. To see the whole shape, the full-screen map (with its own switcher) is where you zoom and pan.
- **Full-screen candidate map (Oct 2):** the place card (place over its region, the area card's look) sits at the top right, as on the main map; the legend runs down the top left. It replaces the "Estimated ignition point / Within ±375 m" title there.
- **Stage-change chips (Oct 2, standing):** a chat suggestion that moves the fire to its next stage (Move to Being resolved, Concluding, Surveillance) leads with a •→ icon (a solid dot, then a right arrow), so it reads apart from questions and other actions.
- **Trophy card (Oct 2):** the logo, the trophy circle and the sparks are white (were the hi-vis yellow); the action is a fully rounded pill, 48px, like every text button.
- **Back keeps the scroll inside the chat too (Oct 2, fix):** going from a screen within the chat (resolution summary, team list) to a person and coming back with Back (or the grabber) returns to the same scroll position; the rule "Back keeps the place, anything else opens at the top" now holds for in-chat screens as well as for pages.
- **Report night ignition (Oct 2, replaces the tag on the time card):** its own card under the time card, 8px apart, the same shape as the GPS and time cards (white, 16px corners, 56px), its text and a shield icon in the Resolving blue (#1F64A6).
- **Report night ignition (Oct 2, later; replaces the card):** a floating glass pill like Please dispatch resources, just below it (or in its place when there is nothing to dispatch), in the Resolving blue with the moon on the right; Situation tab only. Both pills now read in Portuguese too.
- **Login version date (Oct 2, automatic):** the date under "Alpha version" follows the app's last change: it reads version.txt, which every design change rewrites (the automatic data updates leave it alone). Day, short month, year in the app language ("2 Oct 2026", "2 out 2026").

- Dispatch, Crews tab of a confirmed fire: while nothing is chosen, the AI suggested pack card sits above Add resources (8px gap), same card as in the configuration view.
- Floating pills ("Please dispatch resources", "Report night ignition") share one width: min(320px, 100% − 64px), content centred.

- Confirm fire? dialog: buttons read "Dismiss" / "Confirm" (the title already names the fire; redundant-words rule). Standalone buttons with no such title (drone view, chat chips) keep "Dismiss fire" / "Confirm fire".

- Crews tab: no mini KPI tiles (and no "Estimate" label). The crew figures (Crews, Crews need relief, Tenders low on water, Relief due within 3 h, Fresh crews, Longest shift, Water in tanks, Aircraft dropping) are optional cards in Situation's Key figures, off by default; the user switches them on from the + tile. Vehicles and People are not repeated there (Key figures already has Vehicles and Personnel). The Crews tab's red dot now means only "confirmed fire with no crews".
- Confirm fire? dialog: "Chat with the team first" spans the full width, 48px tall like the buttons below (17px semibold, centred, chat icon kept).

- Full-screen map legend (stacked chips, every map): all chips share one width, set by the longest; counts right-aligned at the chip's end (8px min from the label).

- Map legend, layer off (every map): the chip keeps its normal marker shape, drawn in middle grey (#8E8E93) at 20% opacity (no colour), and the chip background is 5% darker (#E6E6EB; dark theme #222225; 10% was too dark). Layer on: unchanged.

- Chat badge (every header): counts unread messages only in the chats the chats list shows — open chats in the selected area, plus direct messages. Chats from another area no longer add to it (a Portugal view showed 18 from Los Angeles chats over an empty list).

- Chats and notifications follow the selected region: the chats list, its counts and every chat badge show only that region's chats; direct messages belong to their country/state (Portugal never shows Brazil's or the US's). One shared filter, __wfChat.inScope. Notifications are already built from the region's candidates and fires.

- All US states enabled in the region picker (same NIFC fires, FIRMS candidates and OSM stations the app already loaded for California, so no extra download). A single-country view (Portugal) costs nothing more; the whole-US view is about as heavy as the whole-Brazil view.

- Map legend: at least one layer always stays on; tapping the last one that is on does nothing (every map).
- **Login:** the build stage sits on one line above the demo note at the bottom: "Alpha version." in bold hi-vis yellow, then "Last update: 2 Oct 2026" in white (16px, the date of the last design change; no longer under the logo). The note starts with "Limited audience.". The empty Username field uses the field size (17px, like Password). The hint under the form reads "Tap username to choose a demo profile." (sentence case).
- **Glass over maps (Oct 2, every map):** everything that sits over a map is frosted glass, so the map shows through softly: legend chips and weather chips (the surface colour at 62%, an unselected chip a shade greyer at 62%), the location cards (place title card, the fire's full-screen header, the pin's place card) in the same glass, the tooltip cards (white at 72%, blur 20px), all with blur 16px and saturate 180%. Dark mode: the dark surface at 74%, so light text keeps its contrast. The round map buttons keep the dark glass, now lighter: black 50%, blur 16px, saturate 180%.
- **Share (preferences, Oct 2):** before Chat settings, a "Share" group label with one white card, "Share with other people" and the forward chevron. A tap opens the phone's share sheet with the app's link (where there is no share sheet, the link is copied and the card reads "Link copied" for a moment).
- **Remove buttons lighter (Oct 2, everywhere):** the red tint behind Remove is 10% (was 15%), the confirm dialog's Remove 9% (was 14%); dark mode 10% and 15% (were 15% and 20%). Red text unchanged.
- **Re-centre only re-centres (Oct 2, every map, in the page and full screen):** it moves the view back to where the map opened and nothing else: no legend change, no markers added or removed. It always takes its own tap (while the map is centred it just does nothing, never letting the tap fall through to the map or a chip). For a second after it, layer toggles are ignored. Legend chips toggle only on a real tap (the finger moved under 8px), so a pan that starts on a chip never switches a layer.
- **Login photo zoom (Oct 2):** each background photo zooms 10% faster than before (to 133% over its 18 s on screen, was 130%); the loading screen carries on at the same new pace, so the handover never jumps.
- **About (Oct 2):** a screen of its own (About.dc.html), opened from Login ("About" as text only in the hi-vis colour, no container, centred 24px under the demo note and above the photo credit) and from the last row of the user preferences (a 52px pill on the grey of the switcher and search field, the app's symbol then "About" centred, no chevron; the symbol's eye blinks every 4 s; back returns to the preferences still open). Header: back on the left, the logo as in the preferences, centred; its eye blinks every 5 s. "About" in Title M, centred, then two parts, each under a group label (no "How it started" part; every text kept to one or two short sentences): **Goal:** ("In a wildfire, every first second counts. Our question is:" then, on its own line in bold, "Is it possible to reduce the time between ignition and action?"; the flow Detect → Confirm → Deploy; the chat band as a question, "Can one chat per incident keep everyone aligned?"), **Where we are:** (labels end with a colon); **Where we are** (stages with a check when done, the current one with a solid dot in the hi-vis colour and a "Now" tag, then the stage and what the expert interviews are for, ending on its own line with **What are we missing?** in bold; examples of the government bodies: Proteção Civil in Portugal, Cal OES in California). It ends with a simple "Thank you.", the authors as a short centred line in normal text, 17px (three lines: "Project by", "Anastasia Osling, designer", "Nuno Rogério, designer"; no company), and the build line from Login. Anastasia is always named before Nuno. English, Portuguese and Japanese. All of its text is normal text (17px, black), the flow steps included; the only grey text is the group labels and the build line.

- **Find a place on the main map (Oct 2):** a tap on the search button opens the full panel straight away (on the touch itself, so iPhone shows the keyboard) and the tap's own click is swallowed so it can't close the panel that now covers the button. A drag across the button does nothing. Closing always hides the panel fully: it never stays as a top strip that ignores touches.
- **Full-screen area maps, overlays (Oct 2):** the place box (e.g. "Aveiro", with its count) sits top left; the legend column sits top right, chips right-aligned. The place box never runs under the legend (it stops 16px before it). Once the map is close enough for weather, the weather moves to the bottom, left of the round controls: a grid of 44px rows, 8px apart (like mini KPIs), level with the two lowest round buttons. The upper row holds two cells (wind, fire danger), the lower row three (temperature, humidity, air quality); with four values it is 2 + 2. Cells use the legend's frosted chip, content centred. On a map band in a page the weather stays a row of chips under the legend.
- **Login, Log in button (Oct 2):** it sits a little above the middle of the space between the username field (and its hint) and the text block at the bottom (more space below it than above, about 1 : 3, so it reads with the field), closer to the field it belongs to.
- **Build line (Oct 2, Login and About, one shared definition):** two lines: "Alpha version." then "Last update: 2 Oct 2026, at 15:53", the date and time of the last design change shown in the viewer's own time zone and clock style (PT "Última atualização: 2 out 2026, às 15:53").
- **No lone words (Oct 2, About first, apply to body text app-wide when touched):** a paragraph never ends with one word alone on its last line: text-wrap: pretty, and the last two words of longer texts held together.
- **Quick tour (Oct 2, tour.js, shared by Main, Alert, Dispatch and Chat):** started from the "Tour" button at the bottom right of the home screen's bottom blade (a line helicopter, 28px, with "Tour" under it in 13px semibold, level with the blade's title). Each step is a translucent dark-glass bubble (20px corners, blur, white text): the action as its title (17px semibold), one or two sentences (16px), then "3 of 35", End tour (on every bubble) and, where the step needs no tap, a lime Next. A hand-drawn arrow (a slightly bent dark stroke on a white halo, drawn in as the bubble appears) points to the control, which gets a pulsing hi-vis ring; the bubble sits above or below it, 16px from the screen edges. Tapping the pointed control moves the tour on. The route: region, legend, map, list → the most likely candidate (the demo ignition) → its page (confirm tag, full-screen map, drone, projection) → team chat (confirm, configure dispatch) → fire page (AI suggested pack, send orders, return) → chat → fire page (Situation and Crews, drag a card, the + tile) → chat (move the fire forward until it closes) → fire summary → home: notifications, chats, settings and connected data sources. A step whose control isn't on screen after 9 s offers Next instead, so the tour never gets stuck.
- **Quick tour, tour mode (Oct 2):** while the tour runs a soft lime glow frames the whole screen (Siri-like, breathing gently), and only the bubble and the control it points at take touches; scrolling still works everywhere. The bubble's footer: step count, End tour, then ‹ (previous step, going back a screen when needed) and › (next; dimmed on steps that wait for a tap on the control; a named pill such as Start, Fire page, Home or Finish where the step navigates). The legend step points at a hidden (grey) layer chip. A bubble never covers its control or an open map tooltip: it moves to the other side or to the top or bottom of the screen. In the chat the tour plays the fire owner itself (approves air support, moves the stages on, closes the fire) so the user watches the status cards change instead of hunting for replies.
- **Tour button and achievements (Oct 2):** the home Tour button reads "Let's take a tour" (11px semibold) under the helicopter; every 6 s the helicopter dips its nose about 20° with a slight bounce and comes back, icon and label glowing lime while it moves. During the tour, milestones put an achievement tag at the top of the next bubble: a translucent lime pill with a check ("You've confirmed an ignition. Great work!") and under it what comes next ("Now let's deal with the fire."), with a short haptic; shown going forward only. The map step points at a circle well inside the map so its summary panel opens whole, and View on that panel works during the step.
- **Tour glow (Oct 2):** thinner and more transparent: a 1px lime line at 55% with a 14px inner glow at 40%, breathing over 3.6 s between full strength and 60% more transparent.
- **Tour glow (Oct 2, revised):** only along the two sides of the screen, 80% of its height, centred, fading out at both ends; never across the top or bottom, so the status bar keeps the app's colour.
- **Tour and the area picker (Oct 2):** on the region step the area picker can be opened and a region chosen inside it; the picker closes as soon as the tour moves to any other step, so each step's control is in view.
- **Tour, list step (Oct 2):** the highlight stays on the most likely candidate (the list's first row) and never jumps between rows; a tap on any candidate row also opens it and the tour carries on from the ignition page.
- **Tour progress rules (Oct 2):** › stays disabled until the user taps what the bubble points at, and that tap moves the tour on by itself; steps that only explain (no control to press) keep › enabled and their highlighted element doesn't take taps (a card meant to be dragged excepted). After going back with ‹, › is enabled through the steps already done, up to the furthest one reached. The tour's own taps (closing a panel, hiding a layer, closing the drone feed) always go through.
- **Tour, show then tidy up (Oct 2):** the legend step shows the tapped layer for about 2 s, then hides it again and moves on, so the map keeps only ignitions; the drone step plays the live feed for about 4.5 s with a "Live drone feed" bubble at the bottom, then closes it; the projection step never sits under the full-screen map or the drone feed. The ignition page's first step points at the key figures (scrolled into view) and says they can be reordered (press and hold) and chosen (+).
- **Tour entry warning (Oct 2, while the tour is being tuned):** tapping "Let's take a tour" first shows a dark-glass tooltip above the button, like the tour bubbles: "⚠️ Warning: the tour is still buggy", "You might not be able to return. Proceed at your own risk.", then Not now | Start tour.
- **Tour, never locked (Oct 2):** End tour is reachable at every moment: while the tour waits for a step's control to appear, its bubble stays at the bottom of the screen and the screen isn't locked.
- **Tour hidden (Oct 2):** the quick tour and its Tour button are switched off for now (tour.js keeps the work; WF_TOUR_ON = false). Turn it back on once it is tuned.
- **Tour button back, tour still off (Oct 2):** the helicopter "Let's take a tour" button is shown again on the home bottom blade. While the tour is off, a tap opens a dark-glass tooltip above it: "Tour is under development. It will return once we're sure the chopper gets you by safely." It closes with its X or a tap outside.
- **Tour back on (Oct 2, evening):** WF_TOUR_ON = true. The entry tooltip reads "⚠️ Tour under construction. We're still working on it and it has lots of bugs. End tour is always there to get you out." (PT: "Visita em construção…"), then Not now | Start tour. Checked on a phone viewport: all 36 steps walk through to the end, and End tour at each step leaves a normal, usable screen.
- **Chats blade area (Oct 2):** the chats blade uses the same area as the map and lists, so a profile locked to a country (e.g. Portugal) sees its own chats instead of an empty Los Angeles list.
- **Tour helicopter rotor (Oct 2):** the rotor blinks between two moments with no transition, in a continuous loop: full width, then shrunk to 2px at the mast (0.15 s each). Off with reduced motion (rotor stays full).
- **Tour helicopter bucket (Oct 2):** a 0.8px cable (60% transparent) hangs from the belly through the label and below, ending in a small water bucket (line style, faint hi-vis fill). It is a damped pendulum: a gentle sway side to side, and when the helicopter dips its nose the cable and bucket react to the motion, then settle. Glows with the helicopter; hangs still with reduced motion.
- **Quick tour route (Oct 3, supersedes the earlier route):** one ignition only, start to end: intro → the region selector (shown, not opened) → the incidents list (shown, not opened) → the most likely candidate on the map (tap) → View in its map card → key figures → confirm it with the team (chat button) → tap the incident's state → Move to First alert (in the state card) → open the fire (its name at the top of the chat) → fire map full screen → try the map freely (legend, +1/+3/+6 h, drone; › when done) → back to the page → back to the chat → Move to Ongoing (state card, opens dispatch) → AI suggested pack → Send orders → Return → the tour moves the stages on → open the stage stack (the pinned state) → Close fire → the closing checks → fire closed → the fire summary, which ends the tour. If the dispatch plan is still empty at Send the orders, the tour fills it with the AI pack (never a dead end).
- **Chat title (Oct 3):** the incident's name at the top of a chat opens its page (the fire page for a fire, the candidate page for a candidate).
- **Tour guide helicopter (Oct 3, replaces the arrows):** the Tour button's helicopter takes off (growing 20%) and flies to each control to tap, hovering over it with the water bucket pointing at it (the cable a damped pendulum, 0.8px at 60% transparency, the rotor blinking as on the button); it faces where it flies, dipping its nose with speed. With no room above a control it hovers beside it; on steps with nothing to tap it waits above the bubble. Changing screen, it flies out to the left (deeper into the incident) or right (back) and comes in from the opposite side on the next screen. A thin white halo keeps it readable on maps. When the tour ends it lands back on the Tour button (or flies off when ending elsewhere).
- **Tour demo ignition starts fresh (Oct 3):** chats are kept on the phone while a confirmation lasts only until the app closes, so a tour's chat from an earlier session is cleared when the tour reaches the demo ignition; the confirmation always comes first. A dispatch plan already filled skips the AI pack step.
- **Full-screen fire map (Oct 3, supersedes the Oct 1 fire header):** no status band: the place and its county in the compact title box at the top left, the legend in a column beside it at the top right, as on every full-screen map.
- **Night ignition report (Oct 3):** the chat is with a police leader of the force that investigates fire causes there, named with rank and department: US: Captain, <County> County Sheriff's Department (Sheriff's Office outside California); Portugal: Capitão, GNR. Comando Territorial de <distrito>; Brazil: Capitão, Polícia Militar Ambiental; Spain: Guardia Civil SEPRONA; France: Gendarmerie nationale; Italy: Carabinieri Forestali; Greece: Hellenic Police; Canada: Inspector, RCMP. His name line reads rank, name and department (e.g. "Captain Jordan Reyes. San Bernardino County Sheriff's Department"), and so does his photo tooltip.
- **Tour on iPhone (Oct 3):** one tap per step: the tour moves on with the tap passed on to the control (not the haptic layer's own), never blocks touches once it belongs to another screen, and brings a step back if its tap didn't open the next screen within 2 s. The tour scrolls only containers people can scroll themselves and keeps the page and clipped frames at their origin, so the header row (back) never slides out of reach.
- **Tour bubble (Oct 3):** draggable (a light grabber at its top; drag anywhere but its buttons); it stays where it was put for that step, kept on screen. While waiting for a control it waits just above the middle of the screen, clear of the header and of the controls that sit low.
- **Tour plays the fire owner (Oct 3):** in the automatic steps it presses only Approve, Confirm fire, the stage chips (Move to…, Air support) and Close fire, and points only at the control it is about to press: nothing else takes a tap then (no Dismiss by mistake).
- **Bottom blade list icon (Oct 3):** on the main collapsed bottom blade, a 20px list icon in middle grey #8E8E93 (three thin lines, 1px on screen, with round dots) sits right after the ignition candidates count, saying the blade opens the incidents list. The same icon follows the area line under the top blade title (e.g. California), which opens the area picker.
- **Tour guides (Oct 3):** the guide is a light-blue translucent circle (80px, #5AC8FA at 26% with a 1.5px edge at 70%, a slow gentle pulse) centred on the control to tap; it lets every touch through, so the control under it works as it is. The Tour button shows the same circle (28px) instead of the helicopter. The "Chopper tour test" demo profile (administrator access) keeps the helicopter tour unchanged: the helicopter on the Tour button, the flying guide helicopter and the yellow frame.
- **Spacing scale (Oct 3, Deepspace):** every padding, margin and gap uses only 4px or a multiple of 8 (4, 8, 16, 24, 32, 40, 48, 56…), app-wide. Applied to all screens: 2–5px became 4, 6–7 became 8, 9–10 became 8, 12 and 14–15 became 16, 18 became 16, 20 became 24, 30 became 32, 52–53 became 56. Hairlines (1px), negative offsets and computed values stay as they are. Where an older entry here names another spacing value, this scale wins.
- **Tour cards (Oct 3):** in the circle tour the information cards are light grey frosted glass (rgba(229,229,234,0.72), blur 24px, saturate 180%, a 1px grey edge; light blue until later on Oct 3) with dark text; secondary buttons a faint dark fill. The Chopper tour test profile keeps the dark glass cards.
- **Tour guide circle (Oct 3, later):** yellow again: the profile's hi-vis yellow at 30% fill with a 2px yellow edge and a faint 1px dark outline for contrast; same on the Tour button. Its pulse grows slowly to +10% (75% of a 1.8 s cycle) and returns quickly to its size. The tour cards are light grey glass.
- **Tour steps (Oct 3):** no step asks you to write to the team; after Confirm fire the tour shows the "You've confirmed an ignition" message and goes straight to Open the fire (22 steps).
- **Tour never leaves you without the guide (Oct 3):** in Try the map, leaving the full-screen map on your own (exit, or turning the phone upright) moves the tour on to Back to the team, skipping Back to the page.
- **Mini card values: three sizes (Oct 3):** a mini card's value uses one of three sizes on the 8px scale, 40, 32 or 24px: the largest that fits with 8px clear on every side of the card and 4px clear of a vertical note (Estimate). If 24px is still too wide, the value wraps onto two lines at 24px within that space. Units keep their size. It wins over the text-size setting and is chosen again whenever the cards change (no other value sizes in mini cards).
- **Fire page band (Oct 3):** the kind reads "Active" (or "Held"), not "Active fire": the page is already a fire.
- **Tour card and glow (Oct 3, later):** the circle tour's card is the dark glass (the chosen look; the light-blue and grey trials are dropped) with a soft hi-vis yellow glow all round, pulsing with the screen-edge glow. The two screen-edge glows are 110% of the card's height, centred on it, and follow it while it is dragged and when it moves between steps. Achievements ("You found your first ignition" and the others) are a solid hi-vis yellow pill with dark text and a soft yellow glow. The Chopper tour test profile keeps its own look.
- **Floating pills stay still when pressed (Oct 3):** Please dispatch resources and Report night ignition are centred with left/right auto margins, never a transform, because a pressed button drops its transform and the pill used to jump sideways under the finger. A tap on Report night ignition goes straight to the police chat, except while the tour runs (not its route: the tap does nothing).
- **Tour guide circle moves (Oct 3):** the circle always marks the next tap: the control on screen, or the card's own Start / Next when the step moves on from the card (it sits over the card, letting the tap through). It never appears straight on its target: at the start it sets off from the Tour button; on a new screen it enters from where it was on the previous screen and moves to its target once the screen has loaded and the target has held still for 300 ms; then it follows it. The Tour button's circle pulses like the guide.
- **Tour glows balance (Oct 3, later):** the card's yellow glow is soft (20px, 36% to 14%); the screen-edge glows are the evident ones (31px wide, a brighter 2px edge line, pulsing between 100% and 52%), 126.5% of the card's height, centred on it.
- **Tour start (Oct 3):** the Tour button starts the tour straight away (no warning dialog first). The first card is titled "Let's deal with a fire ignition now" (was "Quick tour"), with "⚠️ Under construction" right under the title in the hi-vis yellow.
- **End tour (Oct 3):** End tour always returns to the main screen; there the guide circle flies from where it was back into the Tour button, shrinking to its size, and the button's own circle takes over.
- **Tour card glass (Oct 3, later):** slightly darker and see-through: rgba(10,10,12,0.72) with a lighter blur (12px, saturate 160%), so the screen underneath shows softly through it. Circle-guide profiles only.
- **State card moves the incident (Oct 3):** the chat's pinned state card is where the status changes: tapped, it expands (time in this stage, since detection) and shows a full-width primary "Move to <next stage>" button. It runs the stage's own step: Confirm (candidate → First alert), Configure dispatch (→ Ongoing, opens the dispatch screen), the plain move for later stages, and the closing checks (→ Closed). The card folds back when the button is tapped. The suggestion chips still work too. The tour uses the state card for confirming, dispatching and closing.
- **Edge glow magnetism and shape (Oct 3):** the two screen-edge glows are 110% of the card's height (was 80%, before that 145.5%), centred on it, softened by a 2px blur, and shaped like a lens: widest at the middle, tapering to a 4px line at their top and bottom ends. Magnetism: as the card moves towards one side, that edge's glow widens (up to +133%: +55%, +20%, then +25% more), brightens (+20%) and blurs more (+20%) while the far one narrows (-60%), dims (-45%), shortens (-20%, still centred) and blurs a little more (+10%), reaching full effect when the card is dragged to its 8px limit, following drags live.
- **Edge glow pulse (Oct 3, later):** more evident and still smooth: over the same 3.6 s ease-in-out cycle each glow fades to 30% and narrows to 78% from its screen edge, then returns.
- **Tour shows the controls first (Oct 3):** before the map pick, the tour points at the region selector, the incidents list, the chats, the notifications and the preferences in turn; at each, once the circle has arrived, the card and the circle fade away, the blade opens by itself, stays open 1 s and folds back, then the card and the circle fade in again (just to show it); Next moves on.
- **Key figures step holds still (Oct 3):** the card sits low and stays put (no re-placing, no flicker); the page starts from its top and scrolls down by itself, slowly (70 px/s), to its end so every section is seen; any touch, wheel or scroll stops it. The circle stays on the key figures area, kept on screen above the card.
- **Tour card follows the phone's tilt (Oct 3, revised 12:25):** at the start of each step the phone's angle becomes the resting angle; within 10° either side of it the card stays put. Past that, tipping flatter slides it up and more upright slides it down; the angle sets the target speed (36 px/s just past the still zone, 1071 px/s at 40° past it), the card accelerates towards it and decelerates smoothly back in the still zone, always kept on screen. Once the card has been dragged in a step it stays where it was put and moves only by dragging, until the next step, when everything resets. iPhone asks for motion access when the tour starts.
- **Edge glows (Oct 3, 12:07):** both edges 20% wider (base 37.2px) and 20% more opaque, on top of the magnetism.
- **Edge glows (Oct 3, 12:20):** 20% more opacity again (1.44× the earlier base).
- **Edge glows (Oct 3, 12:23):** the near edge is also up to 30% taller and 20% wider again (still centred, still pulsing).
- **Edge glows without the card (Oct 3, 12:45; replaces the full-height version):** whenever the card is away (fading out for a blade demo, or between steps) the edge glows fade away with it, and fade back in when it returns.
- **Tour card inertia (Oct 3, 12:28):** a swipe on the card throws it: it keeps sliding the way it was swiped and decelerates smoothly (about 0.3 s decay), stopping at the screen edges; a touch catches it. Where it comes to rest it stays for the rest of the step.
- **Tilt is vertical only, with a rubber bump (Oct 3, 12:55):** the sideways tilt is gone (it clashed with the screen turning). When the card hits the top or bottom of the screen, sliding by tilt or thrown by a swipe, it bounces back like off rubber: about 30% of its impact speed, so a hard hit comes back further, a soft one barely; it coasts to a stop and then follows the tilt again (one bounce per arrival at an edge; a soft touch just settles).
- **Share link offers the app (Oct 3):** Share with other people sends …/#install (the administrator's invite links do the same). Opened in a browser tab, the sign-in (or main) screen offers the app with the shared confirmation dialog: Android and desktop Chrome, "Install Fire Watch" with Not now | Install (Chrome's own install prompt); iPhone and iPad, "Add to Home Screen" with where to find it and one full-width OK (no page can install there). Once per opened link, never inside the installed app; on Android Chrome opens the link straight into the installed app. The shared dialog takes `cancel: false` for a one-button information dialog.
- **Tour tilt off (Oct 3, 13:32):** the tour bubble no longer moves when the phone is tilted (and no motion permission is asked); it moves only by dragging. Everything else in the tour stays. Switch back with TILT_ON in tour.js.
- **Tour bubble bounce (Oct 3, 14:19):** all four edges behave the same: a thrown bubble stops 8px from the screen edge and bumps back at 30% of its speed, then coasts to a stop. It never dips into or past the 8px margin.
- **Tour blade demos (Oct 3, 17:11):** the guide circle taps the button first (swells 25%, quickly back to its size), then the blade opens, stays 2.5 s and folds back (was 1 s); once folded, the tour moves to the next step by itself.
- **Tour achievement sound (Oct 3, 17:20):** each achievement plays a short bright "prlim" (three quick rising notes landing on a soft bell), with the haptic. Built with Web Audio, no file; the phone's silent switch mutes it.
- **Menu (Oct 3, 17:18, trial):** the profile picture in the top bar is now a menu button (three stacked lines, the same round grey button as chats and notifications), on every screen. It no longer opens a blade: the whole screen slides left and uncovers the menu underneath. On the menu's right edge, a column of round icons (84px wide, level with the top bar's menu button): the menu button, then one icon per section (the profile picture first, then Language, Theme, Text size, Share, Chat settings, Connected data sources, About), and Sign out at the bottom for a quick sign-out.
