The menu opens on the profile; the open section's icon fills with the hi-vis yellow (the picture gets a yellow ring) and its name is the title beside it. Tapping the open section's icon slides the screen back right, leaving only the icon column showing; tapping any icon there opens that section again. The menu button slides the screen all the way back and closes the menu. About opens the About screen.
- **Menu reveal (Oct 3, 17:36):** the menu is always there underneath, fully opaque, already on its first section (the profile). The screen slides out to the left at full opacity and simply uncovers it: nothing in the menu fades in.
- **Menu sections (Oct 3, 17:38–17:39):** with the menu open, choosing another icon slides the current section away to the left, revealing the chosen one already in place underneath (as the screen does over the menu). About is a section like the others: it shows the About content right there in the menu (without its own back button and title), not a separate screen.
- **Menu icon column (Oct 3, 17:56):** 60px wide, 8px either side of the 44px icons (was 84px). Folded to the column, the screen slides back leaving 60px.
- **Menu icon column (Oct 3, 17:56):** the section icons sit centred in the screen's height; the menu button stays at the top (level with the top bar) and Sign out at the bottom.
- **Menu slide motion (Oct 3, 17:57):** the screen sliding over the menu (and a section sliding away inside it) eases in and out: starts slow, accelerates, and slows down as it arrives (0.6 s, cubic-bezier(.65,0,.35,1)).
- **Menu profile icon (Oct 3, 17:59):** the profile's icon is a person outline like the other section icons (yellow fill when open), not the photo; the photo appears in the profile section itself.
- **Menu button stays put (Oct 3, 18:02):** there is one menu button, fixed at the top right above everything (8px from the edge, in line with the menu's icon column). The screen slides out from under it and back under it; the button never moves. It opens the menu and closes it. It hides while notifications, chats, the area picker or a full-screen map are up (their own X sits there).
- **Menu button travel (Oct 3, 18:05):** at rest the menu button sits in the top bar (20px from the edge). As the screen slides to open the menu, the button travels right and locks 8px from the edge, in line with the icon column; closing, it travels back. Two movements at once: the screen slides, the button moves. The button locks like a mechanism, a quick "clack": it speeds into the spot, overshoots 2px, knocks back 1px and settles (0.3 s, starting 0.26 s into the slide), with a light haptic on the lock.
- **Menu motion slower (Oct 3, 18:07):** the screen's slide (and a section sliding away) now takes 0.9 s (was 0.6 s), same ease in and out; the menu button's clack 0.45 s, starting 0.39 s in (was 0.3 s from 0.26 s).
- **Menu: Appearance (Oct 3, 18:10):** Theme and Text size share one section, Appearance (the Aa icon), with a new Spacing switcher: Condensed, Comfortable (as designed, the default) and Spacious. Spacing moves every padding, margin and gap of 16px or more one step down or up the app's spacing scale (16 to 8 or 24, 24 to 16 or 32, ... 56 to 48 or 64), app-wide (prefs.js); the close spaces of 4 and 8px never change, so related things stay together. Switcher options with long names step down to 16px so they fit. The section icons sit centred on the visible screen (allowing for the part of the frame under the browser's bar).
- **Menu section surface (Oct 3, 18:15):** the open section is a surface of its own with the same drop shadow as the sliding screen (0 0 40px at 18%), cast on its right side over the icon column.
- **Menu slide motion, gentler (Oct 3, 18:16):** the slide starts slower and accelerates less: a sine ease in and out, cubic-bezier(.37,0,.63,1), over 1.1 s (was cubic .65,0,.35,1 over 0.9 s); the menu button's clack starts 0.56 s in.
- **Menu surface (Oct 3, 18:17–18:24):** the open section is a white surface (its inner cards turn grey, #F2F2F7, so they still read), with a smaller, lighter drop shadow (0 0 24px at 12%) on its right side only. Opening, the screen slides 32px past the edge so its own shadow never falls on the menu. The same shadow on the sliding screen.
- **Languages (Oct 3, 18:13–18:23):** the Language section is a multi-line switcher: as many options per line as fit, each a round 32px flag (circle-flags, MIT, assets/flags) and the language's own name. The yellow travels to the new choice, shrinking to half its height on the way and growing back as it lands, then the language changes. Languages: English, Português, Brasileiro, Español, Français, Italiano, Deutsch, Nederlands, Українська, 日本語. A language is listed only once it is translated (data/i18n/<code>.json for the new ones); the rest of Europe follows in batches. Role-played crews in the chats reply in the app's language.
- **Menu shadow, subtler (Oct 3, 18:25):** the shadow cast over the icon column (by the open section, and by the screen when folded to the column) is 0 0 12px at 8% (was 24px at 12%).
- **Languages, second pass (Oct 3):** tour texts, chat quick replies and notification texts translated too; a text made of known parts ("Place: Status", "Place. Sentence") is translated part by part in every language.
- **Menu icon column spacing (Oct 3, 18:26):** 16px between the section icons (was 8px).
- **Menu on every screen (Oct 3, 18:34):** the menu button works the same on every screen: that screen slides left from under the button to uncover the menu, and the button closes it back to the same screen (never the map). One menu for the whole app: the main screen's menu, loaded underneath in menu-only mode (Main.dc.html?menu=1, menu.js). Choices made there (theme, text size, spacing, language) apply to the screen above at once.
- **Night moon in dark mode (Oct 3, 18:31):** the moon beside text (lists, detection lines, map popups) turns light at night; on the map's yellow markers it stays dark.
- **Tour achievement (Oct 3, 18:40):** the achievement chip shows last, once the card has settled: it pops in, its background swells 10% and back, and the prlim sound and haptic land at that same moment.
- **Stage tags (Oct 3, 18:50):** the stage icon sits at the tag's left, 8px in; the label stays centred on the whole tag (equal room on both sides). Every stage tag (incident lists, fire page).
- **Language switcher (Oct 3, 18:54):** each option is a flag and the country initials (EN, PT, BR, ES, FR, IT, DE, NL, UA, PL… 日本), the first switcher's formula; the full name is read out by screen readers.
- **About in the menu (Oct 3, 18:44):** scrolls further, so its last lines clear the bottom; in dark mode it takes the menu surface's colour.
- **Translations (Oct 3, 18:56):** stopped at 32 languages; Bosnian not added.
- **No jump on selection (Oct 3, 19:00):** a switcher option's label keeps room for its bold form (an invisible bold copy sets its width), so selecting it never shifts the options around. Language switcher and the menu's switchers; the tabs already did this.
- **Share link (Oct 3, 19:04):** the Share section shows the app's link in the grey copy box (as the invite link): tap it to copy, the copy icon turns into a tick and a dark tooltip confirms.
- **Languages not yet translated (Oct 3, 19:06):** translation work is stopped. The rest of Europe's languages are listed with their flags (Bosanski, Crnogorski, Беларуская, Lëtzebuergesch, Türkçe, ქართული, Հայերեն); tapping one shows a tooltip, "Not available yet", and nothing changes.
- **Mini KPI values (Oct 3, 19:15):** 32px by default in every mini KPI card; a value only steps down to 24px when it doesn't fit (then wraps at 24px as a last resort). Replaces the 40/32/24 largest-that-fits rule.
- **Language flags (Oct 3, 19:17):** 24px round flags (was 32px), 12px in from the option's left edge.
- **Languages trimmed (Oct 3, 19:33):** six languages only: English, Português, Español, Français, Deutsch, 日本語, each with its round flag and its full name (no initials). All other languages, their translation files and flags removed. Supersedes the 32-language and initials notes above.
- **Menu button place (Oct 3, 19:36):** its resting place is set by layout (20px from the edge at rest, 8px with the menu showing, in line with the icon column); the travel and clack are drawn on top from the old spot, so it always ends exactly in line, on every screen.
- **Clean-up (Oct 3, 19:36):** the old drop-down preferences panel's code (profile-picture X positioning, its scrim, grabber and slide values) and the unused language extras are gone. Every asset in the repo is in use (photos, faces and stations files are named in code).
- **Language switcher (Oct 3, 19:39):** two equal-width options per line, in order of how many people speak each language worldwide: English, Español, Français, Português, Deutsch, 日本語.
- **Menu slide 20% faster (Oct 3, 19:39):** 0.92 s (was 1.1 s), same sine ease in and out; the menu button's clack starts 0.47 s in. All screens.
- **Tour in Los Angeles (Oct 3, 19:44):** the tour always runs in California, Los Angeles, whatever the profile's region: starting it sets that region (and lets a profile locked elsewhere see California for the tour only); ending it brings back the profile's own region and choice. In the region step the circle opens the area picker, goes to the selected region (Los Angeles) and taps it, and the picker closes on it.
- **Tour preferences step (Oct 3, 19:45):** the circle opens the menu, goes to About and taps it, holds there for the blades' pause (2.5 s), then goes to the menu button and taps it to close the menu; then the next step.
- **Menu button travel, simplified (Oct 3, 19:49):** the button slides to its place with the screen, same ease and time (0.92 s), and locks with a small clack as it arrives (2px over, 1px back, settle) with a light haptic. Every screen.
- **Menu sections (Oct 3, 19:50):** no shadow over the icon column. Choosing another section, the new one slides in from the right, from behind the icon column, and covers the current one (same ease and speed, 0.92 s); the current one stays in place until covered. Replaces the slide-away-left.
- **Tour Key figures scroll (Oct 3, 19:53):** the page scrolls by itself only until the last row of the first mini KPI cards sits just above the tour card, so the circle stays on them; and only after the achievement chip and its sound have landed (chip and sound first, then the scroll).
- **Menu button speed (Oct 3, 19:58):** the button moves twice as fast as the screen (0.46 s, same ease), then clacks; the screen keeps 0.92 s.
- **Tour blade demos close on screen (Oct 3, 20:01):** after the pause the circle comes back and taps the blade's own close control: the incident list closes on its grabber, chats and notifications on their X; then the next step.
- **Tour suggested resources (Oct 3, 20:06):** a plan left from an earlier tour is emptied when the step starts, so the AI suggested pack always shows; the circle goes to it and, if it isn't tapped, taps it itself after about 3 s (swell and tap), and the tour moves on to Send the orders.
- **Menu button level (Oct 3, 20:08):** the menu button takes its height from its spot in the top bar (measured), so it is always level with the chats and notifications buttons, also in the installed app under the status bar; the icon column's menu button and the section title follow the same height.
- **Menu motion (Oct 3, 20:13):** the screen's slide and a section's slide are 25% faster (0.74 s, same sine ease in and out); the menu button moves 20% faster (0.38 s). Opening, the button moves at once; closing, it waits while the screen slides back and only moves home as the screen's edge reaches the icon column, just before it covers it, then clacks.
- **Likelihood tags (Oct 3, 20:16):** an ignition candidate's likelihood % is shown as a tag with the stage tags' formula (tinted pill, coloured semibold label, one shared width), on a scale of levels: 90–100% red, 75–89% orange, 60–74% amber, 40–59% blue, under 40% grey. One definition (live.js __wfConfTag) for the incident list and the ignition screen's candidate picker.
- **Menu button visibility (Oct 3, 20:19):** it shows with the area picker open (its top row has the same buttons) and hides over the open incident list, as it does with notifications, chats and a full-screen map.
- **Likelihood tags on the map (Oct 3, 20:26):** zoomed in (300% and more), each ignition candidate shows its likelihood tag beside its marker: to the right, or to the left when the right would overlap another marker or tag or leave the map; neither, if both would. The most likely are placed first; markers on the same spot share one tag; the selected candidate has its card instead. The tag is one component (.wf-ctag in prefs.js, colours from live.js) used by the incident list, the candidate picker and the map; on the map it sits on a near-opaque white base with a soft shadow so it reads over the tiles.
- **Menu button motion (Oct 3, 20:29):** the button moves with exactly the screen's motion (same ease, same 0.74 s): opening, together with the screen; closing, the same motion started only when the screen's edge reaches the icon column. Then the clack.
- **Menu sections (Oct 3, 20:32):** the section being covered darkens 10%, gradually, while the new one slides in over it; the icon column is white.
- **Map weather while panning (Oct 3, 20:35):** the weather chips stay up while the map moves: the last place's weather shows until the new place's has loaded. They hide only when zoomed out of range.
- **Likelihood tag on the ignition map (Oct 3, 20:43):** the candidate's map on its own page always shows its likelihood tag beside the pin (right, or left near the map's right edge), whatever the zoom. Same component and colours as the incident list.
- **Likelihood scale (Oct 3, 20:47):** blue only under 45%; from 45% warm tones rise: 45–59 yellow (#6B5300 on #FFF1B8), 60–74 orange (#8A4B00 on #FDE1B8), 75–89 deep orange-red (#A8300F on #FCD9C9), 90–100 purple (#6B2FA3 on #EADCF8); under 45 blue (#1F64A6 on #E3EEFB). Opaque backgrounds with their own deep text colour, all 5:1 or more, like the stage tags without the icon. Supersedes the 20:16 scale.
- **Map tags for twin markers (Oct 3, 20:48):** two candidates on (nearly) the same spot each get their tag, one on each side (the first right, the other left), never overlapping.
- **Language selection (Oct 3, 20:50):** the chosen language is always marked: the yellow is placed on it whenever the section shows (first open, back from another section or screen); each option fills its cell, so the yellow covers the whole option.
- **Spacing switcher icons (Oct 3, 20:52):** the Spacing options are icons, not words: three same-size squares, close together (Condensed), a little apart (Comfortable), far apart (Spacious); the names are read out by screen readers. A switcher option can be an icon (o[2]) with its name as the label for accessibility.
- **Menu button speed (Oct 3, 20:54):** the button's move is twice as fast as the screen's slide (0.37 s, same ease); closing, it starts only when the screen's edge reaches the left of the icon column, then clacks.
- **Menu icon spacing fixed (Oct 3, 20:55):** the icon column always keeps 16px between its icons; the Spacing preference never changes it.
- **Tour auto-scroll speed (Oct 3, 20:57):** one speed for every scroll the tour makes by itself: 104 px/s (49% faster than before), with a gentle start and finish, stopping at any touch. Bringing a target into view, scrolling a chat to its end and the Key figures scroll all use it.
- **Tour Key figures scroll end (Oct 3, 20:58):** the page scrolls until the Key figures section (its label) sits at the top, just under the fixed header, so the whole group of cards shows. Supersedes the 19:53 end point.
- **Dark theme markers (Oct 3, 21:02):** candidate markers (map, legend dot, ignition pin, the Tour button's circle) fill the yellow at 80% at night so they stand out on the dark map as much as by day; their rim and moon stay dark on the yellow, and the pulse ring turns yellow so it shows on the dark map.
- **Count badges (Oct 3, 21:04):** no outline; they sit 4px above and 6px beyond the button's corner, inside the screen, so WebKit never clips them at the top.
- **Tag contrast check (Oct 3, 21:07):** every stage tag and likelihood tag measured against WCAG 2.2 (1.4.3, 4.5:1 for text): all pass, 5.1:1 to 10.8:1, in both themes (opaque light backgrounds). The fallback stage tag colours (used when a fire has no stage) were tinted see-through backgrounds that failed on the dark theme; they are now the stage tags' opaque pairs.

- Stage tag label (Oct 3, 21:09): the icon sits 8px in from the left; the label is centred in the space between the icon and the tag's right edge (32px left / 8px right padding).
- Likelihood tags on the map (Oct 3, 21:12): every candidate shows its tag, including the selected one. A tag goes on the side that is clear and inside the map. If one side has no room, it goes on the other. If neither side is clear, it takes the side with the fewest overlaps, and tags may overlap. A tag is never hidden.
- Dark theme off (Oct 3, 21:13): every screen is light and the Theme choice is hidden from Appearance. The dark styles stay in prefs.js (DARK_ON) for later.
- Menu section switch (Oct 3, 21:14): the section being covered darkens 24% (10% + 14%) as the new one slides over it.
- Achievement order (Oct 3, 21:16): the "prlim" sound (and its haptic) comes first, before anything changes on screen. The achievement card appears 450ms later, and the chip pops last with its 10% swell.
- Chat lists (Oct 3, 21:19): in the chats blade and on the Chats screen, each chat's stage shows as the shared stage tag, in the same format as the incident list. Each row has three lines. The first holds the place in bold, with the tag on the right, all tags the same width. The second holds the last message at full width, up to two lines. The third holds the unread line, with the time and unread count on the right. Direct messages show no tag; their region opens the message line instead.
- Menu sections (Oct 3, 21:27): a section switch starts moving only once the new section has rendered off to the right, scrolled to the top, so nothing changes or settles while it slides in. Every section keeps 16px padding at the top, left and bottom whatever the spacing preference: the title sits 16px down, level with the menu button. Items sit directly on the white panel, with no grey cards or containers around them; their design will come later. A long title wraps to two lines instead of being clipped.
- Menu button (Oct 3, 21:36), locked: its move is 50% faster (0.25s). Everything else stays as it is, including the close timing that waits for the screen to reach the icon column. When the button arrives at the open spot, its three lines turn into an X. Tapped to close, the X turns back into three lines at once. The icon change is a quick 0.2s morph of the vector lines: the outer lines rotate into the X and the middle line fades.
- Round buttons (Oct 3, 21:41): the top-right group (chats, notifications, menu) and the dark map controls line up 16px from the right edge of the screen. Round icon buttons sit 16px apart, light or dark, in a row or a column: top bars on every screen, the map control column, and the drone and full-screen pair. The menu button rests 16px from the edge (8px with the menu open).
- Menu slide speed (Oct 3, 21:44): the screen sliding away and every section sliding in share one speed, 40% faster: 0.53s (was 0.74s), same ease. The menu button keeps its locked 0.25s move. Its close still waits for the screen to reach the icon column.
- About in the menu (Oct 3, 21:45): the embedded About fills its frame exactly, so it scrolls to the end.
- Round button spacing is fixed (Oct 3, 21:47): the spacing preference never changes the 16px between round buttons or their 16px from the right edge. That covers the top-bar groups and the map controls.
- Spacing icons (Oct 3, 21:48): two rows of three squares, so the icons show vertical spacing as well as horizontal.
- Menu sections all behave alike (Oct 3, 21:49): a section shown in a frame (About) stays visible while the next one slides over it. Its copy shows a still snapshot of the page at the same scroll position and darkens like the others. About loads as soon as the menu opens, so it slides in already filled.
- Share = invite people (Oct 3, 22:00): for the administrator, the Share section holds the invite block (Invite people, Create invite link, the copy box and Share), and it no longer appears in Chat settings. Other roles, who can't invite, still see the app link to share. The key note points to Chat settings.
- About while covered (Oct 3, 21:59): the snapshot always hides About's own header and back button, and the slide waits up to 0.5s for it. The embedded About URL carries a version, so phones load the current one.
- Incident chat team (Oct 3, 22:03–22:04): before the fire is declared, a candidate's chat holds the leads of the four nearest stations. Once dispatch orders go out, only the leads of the stations sent stay; the others leave with a line in the chat ("left the chat: station not sent"). The member count therefore follows the stations chosen. Leads are named by their real title: Captain in the US, Crew chief (Chefe de equipa) elsewhere. Older chats are brought into line when the app opens.
- Back (Oct 3, 22:06): going back, the screen you return to slides in from the right over the current one, which darkens 20% as it is covered. It works exactly like the settings sections: 0.53s, same ease, through a cross-document view transition. Other screen changes keep their dissolve.
- Sign out (Oct 3, 22:07): asks first in the bottom dialog ("Sign out?", Cancel | Sign out), from the icon column and from the profile, on every screen. Dialog button pairs stack full width when a label would wrap.
- About is a real menu section (Oct 3, 22:13): it is built into the section like the others, with no frame, so it scrolls and is covered exactly like them. Its header, title and containers give way to the menu's section structure.
- Stage marker in screen headers (Oct 3, 22:14): the candidate circle or fire dot in the stage switcher at the top shows at map marker size (24px box, 2px rim).
- Stage chip in the fire header (Oct 3, 22:14): the 32px left padding belongs to the chip's own style, so the icon never overlaps the label.
- Loading after sign-in (Oct 3, 22:16): "Loading live data" sits just under the counter. The logo and name stay exactly where they were, and the sign-in line fades out in place.
- Drone feed on the full-screen map (Oct 3, 22:09): it drags like the tour cards. It follows the finger after 6px, glides and slows down when thrown, bounces back gently from the 8px edge with no magnet, and stays where it lands.
- Language picker (Oct 3, 22:11): languages show by their own names only (endonyms: English, Español, Français, Português, Deutsch, 日本語), centred, with no flags, because a flag stands for a country and one language is spoken in many. Each option carries its lang attribute for screen readers. The flag files were removed.
- Covered sections darken (Oct 3, 22:20): when the screen slides back over the menu (folding to the icon column or closing), the section darkens 20% as it is covered, like a section switch. When the screen slides away again, it is uncovered already at full brightness.
- Stage tags always agree (Oct 3, 22:23): a fire's tag names its stage on the app's spectrum (First alert, Ongoing, Crews on scene, Resolving, Concluding, Surveillance, Closed), so words, colour and icon always match. The feed's own wording, e.g. "Not contained", spans several stages and used to show different colours under the same words. The same applies in the incident list, the fire page chip and big value, map tooltips and Projection.
- Tags fit their container (Oct 8, app-wide): a tag is never wider than the card, band or row it sits in. The full-screen map's stage tag hugs its own stage name (no room kept for the widest stage, now that the stack is gone) and is capped to what its title card can be beside the legend, still 8px from the card's left, right and bottom. Every tag (qualifier/stage tags, likelihood tags, station tags) gets max-width 100%; a label too long for the room ends in an ellipsis instead of spilling out (prefs.js).
- Candidate map tooltip (Oct 3, 22:26; 22:33): the "Likelihood" label stays on top, with the shared likelihood tag (e.g. "61%", coloured by the scale) 4px under it in place of the big number. The tag sits 16px from the place, and the place, region and View keep their spacing. The card is about 50px shorter (183px instead of about 236px).
- Swipe up ends the tour (Oct 3, 22:32; 22:36): only a very fast upward swipe (over 1800 px/s, mostly vertical) ends it; normal throws glide and bounce. The card flies to the top edge of the screen. There it slows a little (to 55% of its speed) and slides on into the edge, cut flat along it as if swallowed, until it has gone, with a light haptic. The tour then ends as with End tour: the guide circle flies home and the region from before the tour comes back. Throws in other directions still glide and bounce as before.
- Back (Oct 3, 22:41): the screen you return to (e.g. the map) slides in from the left over the current one, which darkens 20%.
- View on a map card (Oct 3, 22:41): the screen splits in two at the marker's height. The top part slides up and the bottom part slides down (0.53s, same ease), uncovering the incident's screen underneath, which starts 20% darker and brightens to normal as the parts leave. It is built with cross-document view transitions (pageswap and pagereveal). Returning from the phone's cache restores the screen whole.
- Helmet camera (Oct 3, 22:50–22:51): the fire screen's map has a camera button (camera icon, after the drone button) that opens a firefighter's live helmet camera, built like the drone feed. It comes out of the button into the map band, 358×202 with rounded 16px corners, "Live. Helmet camera" plus who wears it (the lead of the first station sent), X to fold it back and maximize to fill the screen. It drags like the tour cards. The clip (assets/helmetcam.mp4, 20 s, twice the drone loop) shows a large night fire about 50 m away on a ridge: burning trees, smoke, embers drifting, firefighters hurrying past, and an engine with flashing red and blue lights washing the scene. The camera bobs as the wearer walks toward the engine. It is generated by tools/helmetcam.py. The same feed can be pulled from an incident chat: a camera button left of the message field (fires with crews out) shows another station lead's helmet camera. Shared widget: feedcam.js.
- Icon column (Oct 3, 22:57; removed 23:20): no shadow.
- Maximized feeds (Oct 3, 23:00): the strips beside the app's frame turn black and the menu button hides, so no light lines or extra button show at the sides.
- Stage chip padding (Oct 3, 22:57): the header chip's 32px left padding beats the older 16px chip rule, so the icon never overlaps the label.
- Fire screen feeds (Oct 3, 23:03–23:09): the drone button opens the drone over this fire (assets/dronefire.mp4, 20 s loop, generated by tools/dronefire.py). It is a top view at night from above the helicopter: the fire front across the slope, burnt ground and embers, engines on the road with flashing lights and headlight pools, smoke drifting, and a red helicopter passing below the drone that drops its water load on the front, which steams, dims and recovers. The helmet camera button uses a firefighter's helmet icon (not a photo camera), both on the map and in chats. Each feed opens over the map, 16px from its top-left like the drone feed, and the same button closes it again. Top-bar round buttons sit 16px apart on the fire screen too.
- Chat settings (Oct 3, 23:18): no "Chat role-play and key" drawer row and no chevron. Role-play with Claude, the key and its note show directly, 40px under the section title.
- Incident map full screen (Oct 3, 23:13): on the fire and candidate pages, maximizing slides the top blade away whole (no fade) and the bottom panel down. The map's clip and position animate with them (0.5s, same ease), so the full map opens smoothly under them like on the main screen. Minimizing reverses it.
- Header round buttons (Oct 3, 23:25, Deepspace): on every screen the back button and the top-right round buttons (chat, notifications, menu) sit 16px from the top, as on the main screen (fire, candidate, station, drone and projection screens moved down 8px or 4px), so the menu button never jumps between screens.
- Fire header (Oct 3, 23:25): 16px between the status band and the tabs (was 12px); the band is 56px tall, so the stage tag sits 8px from every edge (was 6px top and bottom).
- Switcher thumb (Oct 3, 23:25, every segmented switcher): the lime sits 8px from the track on all four sides (was 10px), exactly over its option.
- Mini cards (Oct 3, 23:25): key figure cards have 16px top padding like weather and terrain (was 12px); the title keeps a full 40px for two lines, so a two-line title no longer touches its value.
- Fire page footnote (Oct 3, 23:25): the source line sits 56px under the last section (was 72px).
- Map legend chips (Oct 3, 23:25, every map in a page): 16px under the top blade (was 18px), 8px apart (was 6px), 8px padding on the dot side and 16px on the count side (was 10px / 12px).
- Main screen titles (Oct 3, 23:25): the area name in the top blade, its folded area picker title and the bottom blade's summary start 16px from the screen edge, like every other screen (were 24px; the press area keeps its padding, moved out by a -8px margin).
- Tour button (Oct 3, 23:25): its label ends 16px from the right edge (was 20px).
- Profile section (Oct 3, 23:25): 40px from the title to the picture (was 44px); Sign out sits 24px under the profile (was 8px).
- Share link card (Oct 3, 23:25): 16px padding all round (was 8px top and bottom).
- About, phase list (Oct 3, 23:25): the rows' icons line up with the other content at the section's 16px edge (were indented 16px more).
- Pinch zoom (Oct 3, 23:30, accessibility): no screen blocks zooming (no user-scalable=no or maximum-scale in the viewport); `touch-action: manipulation` on the page and its controls stops accidental double-tap zoom.
- Estimate notes (Oct 3, 23:30): the vertical "Estimate" note in key figure cards is solid, not 40% transparent, and is read aloud with the card.
- Darker mid grey on tinted surfaces (Oct 3, 23:30): `#6E6E73` stays only on pure white (5.07:1); text on grey, tinted, orange or frosted surfaces uses `#636366` (token `--wf-mid-grey-tint` in prefs.js): key figure card labels, units and notes, the map credit, the "Key figures" label and hint, the profile's access line.
- Hidden layers and zero counts (Oct 3, 23:30): a hidden map layer chip's text and a zero count use `#636366` (was `#8E8E93`, too faint).
- Login (Oct 3, 23:30): the empty Username field and the Password placeholder are `#3A3A3C` (a disabled Password field keeps its faded grey).
- Spoken names (Oct 3, 23:30): every aria-label starts with the words on screen, in the screen's language. Labels built from parts ("Ignition candidates, 14, hidden. Tap to show") are translated part by part (i18n.js); in Portuguese the stage chip's time reads "há 4 h".
- Closed panels (Oct 3, 23:30): any sheet, dialog, dropdown or menu section with aria-hidden="true" is also inert (prefs.js keeps them in step), so nothing off screen can be focused, read or tapped. Dialogs are named by their title (aria-labelledby); a panel that is always on screen (the main list) is not a dialog.
- Switches and selections (Oct 3, 23:30): switch tracks, on and off, have a 1px `#8A8A8E` inner edge (3:1); the lime thumb of a switcher, the language picker and the tab bar have a 1px `#3A3A3C` inner edge, so the choice never relies on the lime alone.
- Focus ring (Oct 3, 23:30): one ring everywhere, `2px solid #007AFF`, 2px out (`:focus-visible`, prefs.js).
- Small targets (Oct 3, 23:30): the clear-search button keeps its 20px grey circle but has a 24px press area.
- Reduced motion (Oct 3, 23:30): when the phone asks for reduced motion, transitions and animations finish at once (prefs.js); the forest photos and their credit stay still.
- Blade stripes (Oct 3, 23:29, trial): the main screen's location blade has an 8px band of diagonal hi-vis yellow stripes (no black: the blade shows between them, 23:58) along its bottom edge, and the incident blade along its top edge. Each stripe is 24px wide measured along the edge, the band is clipped by the blades' rounded corners, and it rides the blade edge as it moves.
- Map search (Oct 3, 23:26): the map's search button grows leftward into a floating search box (dark map-control glass, 44px tall, fully rounded) that can be dragged anywhere on the map. Typing lists matching places from the third character (OpenStreetMap; offline, the map's own towns) in a glass list under the box, or above it when there is more room there. A tap flies the map to the place, and X folds the box back into its button. Shared widget: mapsearch.js.
- Full-screen map title card (Oct 3, 23:36): its lines size with the text (relative line height, never shrinking) and the card grows with them (min-height), so the title is never cropped under any text size or spacing setting.
- Menu section switch (Oct 4, 00:01): the new section pushes along the icon column. An icon below the open one pushes the new section up from below, and one above pushes it down from above. The old section moves out the other way, darkening 24% as it goes (0.53s, same ease). The covered copy is exempt from the spacing preference, which used to rewrite it a second time and shift it by 1–8px.
- Fire crews match the chat (Oct 4, 00:00): on a fire with a chat, the Crews tab (resources) lists exactly the stations whose leads are in the chat, i.e. the ones sent. Further stations show only when reinforcing.
- Air lead in fire chats (Oct 4, 00:00): from crews on scene (or once air support is requested), the chat includes the air operations lead with their real title: Air Tactical Group Supervisor (US, e.g. CAL FIRE Air Attack) or Coordenador de meios aéreos (Portugal and elsewhere). They join with a line in the chat and never leave when stations change. Older chats get one when the app opens.
- Maps 10% darker in the light theme (Oct 4, 00:06): only the map tiles (the day-tiles filter), never markers, legends or controls.
- Menu section switch (Oct 4, 00:08): the previous section is no longer darkened; it only moves out as the new one pushes in.
- Menu button over panels (Oct 4): it hides whenever a panel covers the top bar, including notifications or chats opened on arrival from another screen (it was overlapping their X).
- Map search on touch (Oct 4, 06:36): a tap on the map's search button opens only the floating search box (never the old full Find a place panel), with the text field focused so the keyboard opens at once. Suggestions show in a panel under it, or above it when there is more room there.
- Empty regions (Oct 4, 06:38): the bottom blade always stays, with its counts (0 ignition candidates, 0 fires) and the tour entry.
- Dialog stripes (Oct 4, 06:42): every bottom dialog and sheet carries the 8px lime stripe band along its top edge, clipped by its rounded corners. Its grabber sits 8px below the band (16px from the top). The menu button sits under dialogs and their glass on the main screen, and hides while a dialog shows on other screens.
- Language picker (Oct 4, 06:44): one centred column of languages with no grey track behind it, options 32px apart, the lime pill travelling up and down to the chosen one.
- Map cards (Oct 4, 06:48): fire and station cards follow the candidate card. The annotation label sits on top (Stage / Status), with its shared tag under it (the fire's stage tag, the station's Available or N fires), then the place in Title XS and its region 16px below. One supporting line follows ("Time active 7h 40m", "Distance 3.2 km"), then View 16px below. The docked tabs, big numbers and crest are gone.
- Split transition safety (Oct 4): if a navigation is cancelled after the screen was split for it, the screen is made whole again after 2.5s.
- **Fire header (Oct 4):** the fire shows only its stage tag (icon, stage, "for 7 h") under the place, left-aligned, with no grey kind band, no dot and no "Active"; a tap still unfolds the stage history from the tag.
- **Fire page Situation / Crews (Oct 4, trial):** the segmented control (the app's switcher, as the projection Now / +1 h) replaces the tabs, 16px under the stage tag; the attention dot stays on the inactive option.
- **Grabbers hidden (Oct 4):** no blade shows a grabber line any more; every blade keeps its behaviours (swipe across its area, drag, tap where the grabber sat to close). Supersedes the visible-grabber rules above.
- **Stripes on top-dropping panels (Oct 4):** the area picker, place search, notifications and chats panels (and the alert's candidates dropdown) carry the firefighter stripes along their bottom edge, as the top blade does; the stripes ride the edge as the panel is dragged.
- **No pulse on ignition markers (Oct 4):** ignition candidate markers (main map, selected ring, detail pin) no longer pulse anywhere.
- **Connected data sources header (Oct 4):** no "Manage data sources" drawer row, count or chevron; the panel's content is always open, and under the title a line reads "**12** data sources connected" (count bold, words regular).
- **Stripes at 80% (Oct 4):** the firefighter stripes (blade edges, panel edges, dialog tops) are drawn at 80% opacity.
- **Maps 16% darker (Oct 4):** the map tiles are darkened a further 16% in both themes (light: brightness 0.9 → 0.756; dark: slope and offset ×0.84). Markers and legends are untouched.
- **No list icons (Oct 4):** the region (top) and incidents (bottom) blades no longer show the list icon after their subtitle; the blades open their lists on a tap or swipe as before.
- **Smart marker count (Oct 4):** the number of things drawn follows the zoom. With over 100 candidates in view (country level, wide regions) no markers are drawn: soft warm blobs show where candidates are more frequent, bigger and stronger where denser, with no numbers. Below that, one marker per 44px (the likeliest), at most 60; likelihood percentages show only when few markers remain (24 or fewer) and the map is zoomed in 300% or more. Fire stations thin the same way (at most about 70 in view).
- **Tapped item survives (Oct 4, Green Valley fix):** the menu loaded in an iframe no longer takes the live flag for a crash and wipes the saved area and the tapped item; every fire or candidate tapped now opens itself, not the first one. A version update reload also keeps them.

## Data sources: what each source brings
- Each source row ends with two groups. "Shows in the app" lists the markers, tags and shapes the source feeds, each with its real symbol (28px in a 32px box) and a 16px label. "Examples" shows real items from the loaded data: bold title, grey subtitle, and the same status or likelihood pill the app uses.
- Group labels are 16px 600 #545458, with 16px above each group and 8px between rows. No invented figures: sources with no loaded item show no example.

## Settings icon column stripe
- The settings (menu) icon column carries the lime stripe down its left edge, full height, 8px wide at 80% opacity, the same shared `.wf-stripes` as the top-dropping panels. It sits in the column's 8px side padding, so the 44px icons do not move.

## Stripe moved to the screen's left edge, 60% opacity
- The settings stripe moved off the icon column to the left edge of the screen: full height, 8px wide, no padding.
- All stripes (shared `.wf-stripes` and the dialog top stripe) now use 60% opacity (was 80%).

## Density multibutton squares
- The squares in the spacing (density) multibutton are drawn with a 1px outline (non-scaling, so it stays 1px at any size).

## Data sources: structure and marker shapes (Oct 4, Deepspace pass)
- Marker symbols in "Shows in the app" and "Examples" are the map's own: an active fire is the solid orange circle (#E8590C; resolved #6F2B06), a candidate the pale lime circle with a dark outline, a night candidate the same with the small moon centred. The flame icon stays only for the fire danger chip, where the app uses it.
- Spacing scale in the list: 56px between sources, 32px between a source's parts (header, "Shows in the app", "Examples"), 8px between label and rows, 4px between name and description. Sources carry no padding or lines, so the gaps alone separate them (no horizontal lines rule).
- Tags in the About stage list use the 13px tag size like every other tag.

## Candidate markers stand out (Oct 4)
- The maps got darker, so the ignition candidate fill goes from 20% to 80% lime by day (it was already 80% at night). Every symbol of a candidate follows: map markers, legend, tooltips, lists, notifications, chats and the data sources.

## Stripes 40% opacity (Oct 4)
- All stripes (shared `.wf-stripes`, the dialog top stripe, the settings stripe) are 20 points more transparent: 40% opacity (was 60%).

## Zoomed out: fewer markers, no blobs (Oct 4)
- The warm density blobs are gone; zoomed out the map shows markers, not haze.
- Fires, ignition candidates and stations thin out together as the map zooms out: wider cells (3x the base spacing at the whole-country fit, 2x, 1.4x, then the base), and lower caps (fires 20, candidates 24 at country level; 40 and 40 a step in; then 90 and 60). Zooming in shows more. The selected item always stays.

## Data sources: title to count 8px (Oct 4)
- The count under "Connected data sources" sits 8px below the title's line (was about 16px visually), whether the title takes one line or two. The title keeps its place level with the menu button.

## Stable markers while panning (Oct 4, standing)
- Panning never makes a marker blink, jump or change. Which markers show depends only on the zoom step (a grid fixed to the map, in power-of-two steps) and never on what is in view: no strides or caps based on the number in view, no reordering. Markers join and leave the list 240px beyond the screen edge, off sight, and keep a fixed order (the data's own order; active fires before resolved).
- Likelihood tags show from a close zoom (4x the fit) and are decided by the zoom alone.
- Station spacing on screen: 140 / 100 / 80 / 64px from the whole country in to the closest zoom, so a screen holds about 100 to 250 at most.

## Group subtitles in all menus (Oct 4)
- One style for the title of a group in every menu panel (Text size, Spacing, Goal, Where we are, Shows in the app, Examples): 16px semibold, mid grey #6E6E73, sentence case, no colon, 8px above what it names. The data sources were the reference; their "Shows in the app" and "Examples" moved from #545458 to #6E6E73.
- In About inside the menu, the goal card has no padding or fill of its own, so "Goal" sits 8px above its text like the other labels.

## Group subtitles 16px; no dividers (Oct 4, supersedes the 15px group titles and the row dividers)
- Every group subtitle (Text size, Spacing, Goal, Where we are, Shows in the app, Examples, and the group titles on the fire, station, drone, dispatch and alert pages) is 16px semibold in mid grey (20px line). 16px is now a text size, used only for these.
- All dividers are off for now: the lines between list rows (About stages, chats, dispatch and station lists), the rule above the dispatch list and the vertical rule between dispatch columns. Spacing alone separates content. The 1px outlines of whole elements (tooltips, pills) stay.

## Menu folded to its icon column: the screen resizes (Oct 4)
- On a phone, when the settings menu is folded to its icon column, the main screen no longer slides left and gets cut. It stays in place and takes the width that is left (60px less): the top blade, the map, the legend and the bottom blade all fit it. Closing the menu extends the screen back to the full width, animated with the same ease. With a section open, the screen still slides away to uncover it. Wide layouts (tablets) are unchanged.

## Map search box and the bottom blade; slower widening (Oct 4)
- The floating map search box stays clear of the keyboard: it is kept inside the part of the screen the keyboard leaves free (16px above it), follows the keyboard as it comes and goes, and returns to the spot it was put in once the keyboard leaves.
- While the search box is up, the bottom blade (ignition candidates and fires) slides away; when the box folds back into its icon, the blade returns.
- When the icon column closes and the screen widens back to full width, the move takes .74s (40% slower than the .53s of the other menu slides).

## Main map default layers (Oct 4, supersedes "only ignition candidates")
- The main map opens with the ignition candidates and the active fires showing. Resolved fires and fire stations stay off until their chips are tapped.

## Tour: swipe up to end, "Tour ended" card (Oct 4)
- Ending the tour by swiping its text card up now needs a swipe 30% faster (2340 px/s, was 1800); anything slower still bounces back.
- When a swipe up ends the tour, a card appears in the middle of the screen: "Tour ended. Restart on the Home screen." (PT: "Tour terminado. Recomece no ecrã inicial."). White frosted card, 28px corners, 17px text and a 44px round X. It fades out after 3 s, or at once with the X. The normal end of the tour does not show it.

## Fire page: projection switcher over the map
- The Now / +1 h / +3 h / +6 h switcher sits inside the map band, on its foot: 16px from the left, right and bottom edges, in the dark map-control style (rgba(0,0,0,.5) with frosted blur), as in full screen.
- The band is 310px tall (234 + 76 for the switcher); the fire (with its +6 h shape) is framed in the clear part above the switcher, and the round map buttons and the attribution sit above it.
- The gap from the map to the next group is 56px. A held fire has no switcher but keeps the same band height.

## Camera feeds: drone and helmet together
- The drone feed and the helmet feed can be open at the same time (a second feed opens in a free place, below or above the first). They never overlap: 8px stays between them.
- Dragging at normal speed: a feed meets the other like a wall and cannot pass through it; a throw bounces off the other feed the way it bounces off a screen edge (30% of the speed back).
- A flick at the speed that ends the tour (2340 px/s) toward the other feed swaps their places: the thrown one takes the other's place, the other takes the thrown one's starting place, gliding together in 0.55 s, with a double haptic tap.
- The same button again closes its own feed only; maximize applies to one feed.

## Menu: the whole screen slides right (main screen)
- Opening the menu with the burger, the whole map screen (map, top bar, blades) slides to the right and rests under the icon column; the menu section is uncovered on the left. Closing, the screen slides back from the right to the left. (Replaces the slide to the left of Oct 3; the 32px extra offset is gone.)
- Choosing a section from the folded column slides the screen right; folding a section to the column (tapping its active icon) slides it back left, to the narrower screen beside the column.
- The screen is cut at the icon column's edge, so the column is always on top and its taps are never blocked. The column wipes in from the right as the screen leaves (0.25 s) and wipes out as it returns.

## About: Goal and Where we are are titles (Oct 4, 15:16)
- The two parts of About, "Goal" and "Where we are", are titles, not 16px subtitles: 20px semibold in black (24px line), smaller than the page title, 8px above their content. Same pattern for any part title inside a menu page.

## Standing rule (Oct 4, 15:20): a change to one case applies to every similar case
- When a decision is given for one screen, apply it to all similar screens in the same pass; when in doubt whether a screen is similar, ask before changing it.

## Ignition candidate page: projection over the map (Oct 4, 15:20)
- Same as the fire page: the Now / +1 h / +3 h / +6 h switcher sits inside the map band, dark map-control style, 16px from the left, right and bottom edges; the band is 310px tall (234 + 76); the pin is centred in the clear part above the switcher, the round buttons and the map credit sit above it; the GPS card is 56px under the map. Without a switcher the band stays 234px.

## Camera feeds: Thermal / Visual and a 4px gap (Oct 4, 15:22)
- Every camera feed (drone, helmet) has the Thermal / Visual switch of the first drone feed, bottom left, 200px wide, same dark glass; Visual is the default. Thermal shows the same feed in false colour (violet, red, orange, yellow, white from cold to hot): there is no separate thermal footage for these feeds.
- Feeds open together keep 4px between them (was 8px), when dragged, thrown, swapped or placed.

## Camera feeds: shrink at the edges, long press, swipe away (Oct 4, 15:26)
- Pushing a feed against a screen edge (dragging past where it stops) shrinks it proportionally, down to 40% of its size (240px of push reaches the minimum). It only gets smaller while pushed; the pushed edge stays 8px from the screen edge.
- When the finger lifts on a shrunk feed, everything goes except the X button (label, Thermal / Visual and maximize are hidden); the X keeps its 44px tap size.
- A long press (about half a second, without moving) on a shrunk feed brings it back to its original size, with a haptic tap.
- A swipe up at the speed that ends the tour (2340 px/s) closes a feed the way it closes the tour: it flies to the top edge and is swallowed by it. If another feed is in its way, the same flick swaps places instead.

## Tour dismissed message (Oct 4, 15:29)
- After the tour card is swiped away, the centred card says "You've dismissed the tour. Restart on the Home screen." (Portuguese: "Dispensou o tour. Recomece no ecrã inicial."), not "Tour ended". It still fades after 3 s or closes with its X. Wording for the same action elsewhere: dismissed, not ended.

## Map with the projection: one formula for fire and candidate pages (Oct 4, 15:46)
- Fire page and ignition candidate page share the same map band: 310px tall, the map itself fills all 310px (nothing clipped), the switcher over its foot 16px from left, right and bottom, the subject (fire with its +6 h shape, or the pin) centred in the 234px clear above the switcher, round buttons 24px above the switcher, map credit above it, 56px to the next group. Do not clip the map to make room: lift the subject (the map's `pin-lift`, or the fire framing).
- Any new page that shows a map with the projection copies this; when one changes, change both.

## Camera feeds: shrinking is a real resize (Oct 4, 15:47)
- A shrunk feed is smaller in layout (not scaled), so its X stays a plain 44px button that closes it; the label, Thermal / Visual and maximize hide as soon as it starts to shrink.

## One camera feed everywhere: the drone feed on the candidate page too (Oct 4, 15:54)
- Supersedes the "drone feed on the candidate screen / full-screen map / maximized" entries: the candidate page's drone button now opens the same floating feed as the fire page and the chats (shared `feedcam.js`), above the map, not in place of it. It drags, throws, bounces and swaps with other feeds, shrinks at the edges, restores on long press, closes with the X or a fast swipe up. The old in-band, full-screen and maximized drone panels of the candidate page are gone.
- Its Thermal / Visual switch uses the drone's real thermal footage (a second video fading in); feeds without thermal footage show the false-colour view.
- Standing rule: similar items look and behave the same; a behaviour added to one (drag, swipe, shrink, close) is added to all.

## Menu on every screen: the whole screen slides right (Oct 4, 16:00)
- Same as the main screen: tapping the burger on any screen (fire, candidate, station, drone, projection) slides the whole screen to the right, tucked under the 60px icon column, cut at the column's edge; closing slides it back left. Tapping the active section icon folds the open menu to the column and back (inverse slide).
- Shared in `menu.js` (`slide()`), so a new screen with the menu gets it by loading the script. On these screens the column mode crops the screen at the column instead of resizing it as on Main.

## Every tap gives its haptic, including blade grabbers and the dim area behind panels (Oct 4, 16:10)
- Tapping a blade's grabber (top or bottom) now ticks like any button. On iPhone the explicit pointer capture the grabbers took on touch stole the tap from the invisible switch that gives the tick; `fit.js` now skips that capture for fingers (a finger is captured by what it touched anyway), so the drag is unchanged.
- Tapping the dimmed / blurred area behind a panel to close it ticks too (those layers carry the class `scrim` or `wf-hap-scrim`; no ripple, just the tick). Any new close-on-tap layer gets one of these classes.
- Standing rule: anything that reacts to a tap gives the haptic; a new kind of tappable element is added to the shared list in `fit.js`.

## Menu folded to the icon column: the screen is resized, not cropped (Oct 4, 16:20)
- With the menu folded to its icon column, every screen (fire, candidate, station, drone, projection) lays out again in the visible width (screen width − 60px), as the main screen does: texts, switchers, bands and maps all fit; nothing is cut at the column. Opening the menu fully still slides the whole screen right, cut at the column.
- How: `menu.js` sets the screen's `--wf-w` and `__wfVP.w` and fires `wf-rail`; screens size their root with `var(--wf-w, 390px)` and maps cap their width to `__wfVP.w` while `__wfVP.rail`. A new screen must use `var(--wf-w, 390px)` for its width (never a fixed 390px) and re-render on `wf-rail`.

## Thermal footage for every camera feed; video call with the police captain (Oct 4, 16:30)
- Every camera feed has real thermal footage behind its Thermal / Visual switch: `assets/helmetcam-thermal.mp4` and `assets/dronefire-thermal.mp4` (false-colour renderings of the visual clips, violet to white), the candidate drone's own thermal clip as before. `feedcam.js` pairs them by file; a new clip needs its `-thermal` pair there.
- Reporting a night ignition: on arriving in the chat with the police captain the floating feed opens by itself as a video call (same widget as the cameras: drag, throw, swap, shrink, long press, swipe up or X to hang up, maximize). Sequence: "Calling…" (rings pulsing around a person glyph), "Connecting…", then the captain's picture fades in and the label says "Live. Video call" with the captain's rank and name.
- The captain's face is one of three (a: African American man, b: European woman, c: veteran man in his 60s), chosen at random once per report and the same in every call of that report (`__wfChat.callFace`). Files: `assets/faces/police-a|b|c.jpg`.
- Entry point on the police chat: the round 44px button at the left of the message bar, the same slot and style as the helmet camera button of the fire chats (a video-camera icon; only one of the two shows, by chat type). It calls the captain again; the feed grows from that button.
- The captain's picture sits whole inside the feed (portrait over a blurred copy of itself), and is also the captain's photo in the chat, so the chat and the call always show the same person. The three portraits are official public-domain portraits of real police chiefs (Wikimedia Commons, see `assets/faces/CREDITS-police.txt`) used as placeholders: swap them for stock photos before any public release.

## Full-screen maps: location on the left, legend on the right (Oct 4, 16:45)
- On every full-screen map the location / title card (name and region or coordinates) sits top-left and the legend top-right; the card's width stops short of the legend, so they never overlap. Same TerrainMap code for the candidate, fire, station and projection pages.

## Fire band: the polygon itself is centred (Oct 4, 16:45)
- The small map on the fire page centres the fire polygon in the clear part of the band (between the legend chips and the projection switcher); the zoom is the largest at which the +6 h shape, measured from that centre, still fits. Previously the centre of polygon plus projection was used, which pushed the current polygon up under the chips.

## Menu folded to the column: the icons work (Oct 4, 16:45)
- The resized screen is also exactly as wide as the visible area (it used to keep its full-width box over the icon column and swallowed the taps), so tapping any icon opens its section and slides the screen back.

## "Night ignition reported" only while the report is alive (Oct 4, 17:45)
- The information that replaces the Report night ignition button shows only while the report chat exists and has not been put away (dismissed). Otherwise the button is back.

## The resolution card shows the forces, not a trophy (Oct 4, 18:00)
- One widget (`trophy.js`, used by the chat's resolved card and the summary's header). No trophy, fireworks or moving nature photos. Background: a group photo of firefighters with their engines behind, by the number of stations that took part: 1 or 2 a small crew, 3 to 5 a medium one, 6 or more a large one (`assets/crews/crew-s|m|l.jpg`, public domain, credits in `assets/crews/CREDITS-crews.txt`, the credit line shown on the card).
- Top: the crests of the stations that collaborated (up to six, then a "+N"), and an air support badge when aircraft helped. Bottom: "Well done" in the accent colour, the fire's name, when it closed and how long it took, and the size class chip. The hazard stripe runs along the bottom edge as on the blades.
- Size classes, one rule for the app (`__wfChat.sizeClass`): in the US the NWCG scale CAL FIRE uses, A up to 0.25 ac, B 0.26 to 9.9, C 10 to 99, D 100 to 299, E 300 to 999, F 1,000 to 4,999, G 5,000 ac or more (from the final perimeter); elsewhere the Portuguese ICNF names: fogacho (spot fire) under 1 ha, incêndio (fire) 1 to 99 ha, grande incêndio (large fire) 100 ha or more. The card shows "Class D. 135 ac" in the US and "Large fire. 135 ha" elsewhere.
- The fire page's Size class card keeps the A to G letter and now carries a tag under it: the class's range in the US, the ICNF name for Portugal and the rest.

## The fire's stage tag goes along into the full-screen map (Oct 4, 18:05)
- On the fire page's full-screen map the stage tag (icon, stage, "for 3 d") and the stack that unfolds from it sit inside the title card, under the place and its region, left-aligned as on the page; the card grows by one row (a placeholder the page measures, `map-tag-w`). Tap the tag to unfold the history, tap outside to fold it. The legend stays on the right.

- **Crews tab guidance (Oct 4):** above the AI suggested pack (all three places it shows: the Crews tab, the plan sheet, the inline plan), while nothing is chosen, a 16px secondary note: "Choose the stations and crews for this fire. Talk with the station coordinators first: the AI pack already counts those talks. As fire owner, you can always choose by hand." It hides with the pack.

- **After sending orders from a fire page (Oct 4):** the button that closes the sent-orders screen names where it goes: "Return to fire" (from the fire page), "Return to chat" (from a chat), "Return" otherwise. The fire's chat is created at once and every coordinator whose station got an order confirms it, one after another, so the chat button's yellow count on the fire page rises with the unanswered replies. The guide wakes on its own (no card, no steps): the light circle sits on the chat button, swells every 2.6 s as if tapping it, stays on it while the page scrolls (the header is fixed), waits while a sheet covers it, and stops for good when the chat button is tapped (`window.__wfNudge`, tour.js). Not shown while the tour runs.

- **Guide edge glow (Oct 4):** when the control the lone guide points at is scrolled out of view, the circle goes and both vertical screen edges glow in the primary lime, pulsing, on the side it is on (top 40% when it is above, bottom 40% when below), until it is back in view.
- **Team video call (Oct 4):** the video-call button (the one the police chat has, in the composer row beside the helmet camera) shows in every team chat while crews are out. The same floating panel rings ("Calling the team…", "Connecting…"), then divides into one rectangle per member of the chat (the coordinators of the stations that got an order, and the air lead): their photo, or their initials where there are no photos, first name bottom left, joining one after another, a lime outline moving between speakers. Up to three columns; a shorter last row shares its width evenly.

- **Close fire is never out of reach (Oct 4):** in Surveillance the Close fire suggestion and the state card's button stay until the fire is closed. Tapping one again brings the "Ready to close" card down to the bottom of the chat (the old one is removed), so replies that pushed it up the chat never leave the owner without a way to close.

- **No separate projection screen (Oct 4; supersedes "Projection screen (Oct 2)"):** the Projection page is gone, and nothing opens it. In the chat's fire card ("Progress and projection") the first group is the fire page's own map band (310px, full width): the perimeter legend, the projection switcher (Now, +1 h, +3 h, +6 h) laid over its foot in the dark map-control style, the drone and helmet-camera buttons, and full screen (with the switcher left of the map buttons). It sits above "Time in each stage". Closing the card resets the horizon to Now and leaves full screen.

- **Resolution card, crests and photos (Oct 4):** crests 120px (smaller only to keep three or four across when there are many), no circle behind them, a soft drop shadow (0 offset), every station's crest and the air team's badge always visible. An aerial team without a crest of its own gets one of five original squadron-style badges, chosen from the fire's name (no real insignia are used). One photo per station (small, medium or large crew by the number of stations, several photos per size, each fire picking its own) plus one aerial photo (3 helicopters and 3 planes) when air support helped; more than one photo makes a carousel: the photos slide to the left on their own, 4 s each, in a loop, with round dots at the top; one photo shows no dots. Credits follow the photo on screen (assets/crews/CREDITS-crews.txt, assets/air/CREDITS-air.txt).
- **Resolution summary foot (Oct 4):** a fixed foot with a line of encouragement (EN "Do not rest on your laurels. There are more fires where you are needed." / PT "Não fiques à sombra dos louros, há mais incêndios onde és preciso.") over a primary "Main screen" button ("Ecrã principal") that goes to the main screen.

- **Overlapping markers (Oct 4, every map):** when markers sit within 26px of each other (a candidate over a fire, two stations), a tap on the spot goes to each in turn, one tooltip per tap, in a fixed order (fires, candidates, stations), then round again, with a light haptic. The first tap opens the one touched. A marker alone behaves as before (tap again closes). It lives in the shared map component, so every map has it.

- **Video calls, hang-up and corners (Oct 4):** in every video call (police captain and team calls alike) the close X is a red round hang-up button (44px, #D70015, white handset turned down, "End call" / "Terminar chamada"), in the X's place at the top right. The panel keeps the video feeds' 16px corners: the call's pictures sit in a clipped layer so no corner ever shows square on iPhone.
- (Oct 4) The night report's video call starts by itself: it waits until the panel script, the chat and the call button are ready (up to ~8 s) instead of one blind 0.7 s try.
- (Oct 4) Video calls: the hang-up is a solid white phone on the red bubble; the chat's call button is a green circle with the same solid phone (not a camera icon).
- (Oct 4) Map: the redraw-throttle / look-ahead / low-res underlay experiment was too sluggish on the phone and is reverted; back to the earlier panning behaviour.
- (Oct 4) A night ignition candidate (moon) keeps its night flag when declared a fire: the fire page shows the moon and the Report night ignition button, using the same night test as the candidate page.
- (Oct 4) Evidence (satellite image, drone feed, citizen photo) opens in the floating card (feedcam.js image mode), like camera and call feeds: draggable, maximizable, X to close; no more dark lightbox. Any future evidence viewer uses the same card.
- **Report ignition on every fire (Oct 4):** the floating "Report night ignition" pill is gone. Every declared fire has a subtle button (full width, 48px) "Report ignition" right under the start time card (night or not; the moon still marks night starts). It opens a chat with the local police and the call starts by itself; once that chat is alive the same button reads "Open report" and opens it. The chat reads "Ignition report" (night fires: "Night ignition report", with the night wording in the messages).
- **Declare fire (Oct 4):** "Confirm fire" is now "Declare fire" everywhere (candidate dialog "Declare fire?" with Dismiss | Declare fire, the chat suggestion, the drone page, the tour, all languages); "Confirming the fire." in chats is "Declaring the fire."
- **Map tiles from MapTiler (Oct 4):** the map picture now comes from MapTiler (its "openstreetmap" style, 256 px tiles, free plan, the key restricted to the app's site in the MapTiler dashboard). The credit on the map reads "Map © MapTiler © OpenStreetMap". Data sources lists MapTiler for the map itself; OpenStreetMap stays for fire stations, place search and routes. The day and night tile filters match "api.maptiler.com". To go back: the two tile addresses in TerrainMap.dc.html (and the filters in prefs.js).
- **Maps menu (Oct 4):** a new menu entry, Maps (folded-map icon, between Appearance and Share), lists the map tile providers under test, MapTiler and OpenStreetMap, each with a toggle. Exactly one is on: turning one on turns the other off, and tapping the one that is on does nothing. The choice is kept on the phone (default MapTiler) and every map follows it at once. Data sources lists the map's source that is in use (MapTiler or OpenStreetMap) plus OpenStreetMap for fire stations, place search and routes. Screens preconnect to both tile hosts so the first tiles arrive sooner.
- **View on a map card (Oct 4, replaces the split):** the current screen slides up and away (0.53s, same ease), the incident's or station's screen pushes up from the bottom over the grey beneath (dark theme: black). Done in plain script (fit.js, no view-transition feature needed), so it works on every phone. Back keeps its own slide-in from the left. Same for every "View" on a map card (candidates, fires, stations).
- **Map looks (Oct 4):** under each provider in Maps, a radio list "Look" of the renders its free plan gives: MapTiler: Standard, Streets, Outdoor, Topographic, Satellite, Satellite with labels; OpenStreetMap: Standard. Choosing a look under a provider that is off turns that provider on (the other goes off). Kept on the phone (wf-maptiles, wf-maprender). Free MapTiler: 100k requests a month, non-commercial, its logo must show on the map (still to add).
- **View preloads the next screen (Oct 4, refines the push-up):** tapping View keeps the card up, its label turns to "Loading…" ("A carregar…") and the destination loads in the background underneath. Only when it is ready does the current screen slide up, uncovering it; the destination starts 20% darker and brightens to normal as the screen leaves (0.53s, same ease). Then the real page opens. If it has not loaded within 2.5s, the earlier behaviour runs: the screen slides up and the destination pushes up from the bottom.
- **Ending a video call (Oct 4):** while the call card is up, the green call button in the chat turns mid grey (#8E8E93). Tapping the red hang-up button turns it green at once; the card swells 10% about its centre and settles (0.2s), then scales down into the green button as before. Same for police and team calls.
- **View: the target pushes up (Oct 4, replaces the slide-up with the grey gap):** no grey screen between. The destination screen, loaded in a frame while the card says "Loading…", rises from the bottom over the current screen (0.53s, same ease), which darkens 20% beneath it. If it needs more time it starts from grey and its content fades in while it moves up. When it has covered the screen the real page opens in its place.

## Fire stage tag: full width (Oct 4)

- Back to a map: any open tooltip card closes on return from the page cache (pageshow), so no card is left active or 'Loading…' (Oct 4).
- View push-up arrival (Oct 4, 21:50): the risen screen's last picture stays until the real page has drawn itself (its plain loading surface gone), then the real page appears over it in a 0.2s dissolve between identical pictures; no blank flash when the new screen settles.

The chat's Move the fire on step is no longer in the tour.
- **Tour flow (Oct 5, 02:01):** … Open the fire → Use the AI suggestion (the fire page opens on Crews with nothing assigned; Add resources is mentioned for a manual dispatch) → Send the orders → Back to the fire (the order dispatch shows; Return to fire lands on Situation) → Back to the team chat → Move the fire forward (the person taps the state and Move to the next stage, stage after stage) → Close the fire → Closing the fire → The fire summary (tap the card) → Explore the summary (Finish). 19 steps; the automatic stage playback and See every stage were removed.

A fire page opened from its chat title also returns with Return to chat after sending orders.
- **Tour cursors (Oct 5):** Send the orders points at the Send order button on screen; Move the fire forward points at the Move to suggestion chip (the state panel's Move button only when no chip is offered). Move chips use the stage names (Move to Resolving, Move to Surveillance).

- **Achievement tag (Oct 5, 02:26):** the sound plays the moment the tag appears, everywhere. On the same screen it pops with the sound; when the tap opens another screen, the tag pops on the current card at the tap (with the sound, the one moment iOS allows it) and the next screen shows it already in place. The tag runs the full width inside the tour card.
- **Mini KPI cards (Oct 5, 02:20), app-wide:** 8px padding on all four sides (the title sits at the top padding). The value follows one rule: it starts at the Likelihood card size (32px value, 16px unit) and shrinks 1px at a time until value + unit fit inside the 8px padding (4px clear of a vertical Estimate note); below 24px it wraps onto two or more lines instead, and only a single word still too wide keeps shrinking (13px at least). Text values (Relief, Fuel) follow the same rule as numbers. Replaces the earlier 32/24 steps.
- **Evidence thumbnails (Oct 5, 02:22):** a tap on an evidence thumbnail (or its card) opens it in the floating feed container (the camera feed widget), with the picture inside. Fix: the tap reached both the thumbnail and its card, so the feed opened and closed at once; one tap now opens it once.
- **About page from the login (Oct 5, 02:39):** brought in line with the menu's About panel: the title now has its description ("How the app works, where it stands and how it started."), 40px above the first group.
- **Login slide reveal (Oct 5, 02:36):** tapping About or the Username field slides the whole login screen (photo included) away to the left at full opacity, uncovering the target already in place underneath: the About screen (loaded underneath first, then the real page takes over) or the profile picker (now a solid app-grey screen, not glass). Closing the picker slides the login back over it from the left; back from About slides it in from the left as on every back. Ease in and out, 0.5s.
- **Login stripes:** the firefighter stripes (shared .wf-stripes: primary-colour diagonal bars at 40%) run along the very bottom edge of the login screen, on the photo layer.
- **Mini KPI values, fix (Oct 5, 03:00):** the fitted size now lives in one style sheet keyed to each value (data-wf-kid), so a card redrawn by live data no longer drops back to a stray old size (Nearest crew, Temperature, Fuel moisture, Spread rate looked tiny). Measured with element boxes in screen pixels (page zoom and the vertical Estimate note's real width included), never a text range. Same rule as before: 32px, shrink to 24px, then wrap.
- **Ignition candidate KPIs:** Nearest fire removed from the cards and from the + picker (it belongs on the station screen, not on a candidate).
- **Profile picker (Oct 5, 02:40):** Create profile stays fixed at the top of the picker; only the profiles scroll under it.
- **Loading screen stripes:** the photo loading screen also carries the firefighter stripes along its bottom edge, like the login.
- **Settings menu order (Oct 5, 02:50):** Profile, Share, Language, Appearance, Maps, Chat settings, Data sources, About. Share uses the chain link icon.
- **Visit page links (Oct 5, 02:45):** every map engine and every data source ends with "Visit page →" (16px, grey, arrow on the right), opening that source's own site in a new tab.
- **Key failure dot:** when a key fails to load (MapTiler's, checked once per session and on every save; the Anthropic key's last call), its menu icon (Maps, Chat settings) shows an 8px red dot at its top right, the same attention dot as the tabs; the icon's label says "Key failed".
- **MapTiler key (Oct 5, 02:48):** the MapTiler engine has a Key block like the role-play key: the app's shared key by default, or your own pasted and tested (kept on this phone only). Save and test, then Remove | Replace key (both ask first); the note says whether it works or why it failed.
- **View push-up, no blank (Oct 5, 02:52):** the next screen rises only once it is drawn on the rising panel (never a blank panel; 2.5s fallback). On arrival, a still copy of the risen screen (its markup and styles, no scripts) covers the real page from its first frame and dissolves in 0.2s once the live page has drawn itself, so there is no blank screen or flicker even where view transitions are not supported. Just the push-up.
- **Rule: the achievement sound plays when its tag animates (Oct 5, 03:12).** When the tap opens another screen, the tag animates on that screen as it rises (the next screen preloaded on the push-up panel shows the tour card there), and the sound plays at that moment (played by the screen that had the tap, which iOS allows); the screen change waits for it, and the arrived screen keeps the card in place with no second entrance.
- **Tour, declaring (Oct 5, 03:15):** "The incident's state" step is gone: the state card opens by itself and the cursor goes straight to Move to First alert. Right after declaring, the state card closes and the tour goes straight to the chat's Configure dispatch card. 17 steps.
- **Tour, dispatch (Oct 5, 03:17):** the fire page always opens on Crews with nothing chosen and the AI suggested pack offered: the demo forgets orders from a tour taken before, and the step keeps the plan empty until the AI pack is tapped.
- **Simulation, one word and one sign (Oct 5, 03:07), app-wide:** no "Estimate"/"Simulation" labels next to values and none written vertically. A widget holding simulated values gets a * in its top right corner (17px, grey, 4px from the top, 8px from the right); a group marked as a whole marks each of its cards. Every screen with any of them ends with "* Simulation" (13px note style) on the left, 56px under the last content and 8px from the bottom. Applied by prefs.js from the notes the screens already write, so new screens follow it too. With the vertical notes gone, values get the full card width.
- **Tour ending (Oct 5, 03:21):** the fire moves forward by itself, stage after stage, with the cursor on its state ("The fire moves forward"); at Surveillance the cursor invites Close fire; the closing checks run with no cursor; then the cursor invites the summary card; the Finish cursor appears only on the summary view.
- **Station screen (Oct 5, 03:24–03:26):** the full-screen station map has no fire legends; its location card leads with the station crest, 32px, on the left (16px before the name). In the header the crest sits left of the title, and the description (the place) sits right under the title (4px), beside the crest.
- **Stage stack fold (Oct 5, 03:27):** folding the stage stack back mirrors unfolding exactly: the same moves played backwards (the last chip out goes home first, easing in; each fades over the last 0.2s of its way home), so open and close read as one gesture.
- **After sending orders (Oct 5, 03:30):** the primary button is "Team chat", with the chat icon left of the label, and goes straight to the fire's team chat (where the leads confirm the orders); no more Return to fire / Return to chat.
- **Resolved fire card (Oct 5, 03:34):** 15% lower (368px, from 432) so it never sits under the scroll fade or the bottom edge: 24px padding, 4px between its lines, the photo credit on one line. Swipe left or right on it to cycle the pictures (a swipe never opens the summary; autoplay restarts after it).
- **Carousel pagination, every carousel (Oct 5, 03:34):** Apple's current style: plain white shapes, no shadow. Pages not shown are 8px circles at 50% white; the page shown is a 20px line with fully rounded ends in solid white; the change animates (width and colour, 0.4s).
- **Summary end (Oct 5, 03:35):** "Do not rest on your laurels…" is centred above the button, which says "Go to main screen".
- **Tour auto-scroll (Oct 5, 03:37):** 40% faster: 146 px/s (was 104).
- **Tour, Send the orders (Oct 5, 03:39):** the step moves on as soon as the orders start going out (the sending screen shows), however the tap reaches Send order.
- **Back from About to the login (Oct 5, 03:41):** a real back: the login (kept in memory) slides back in from the left over the About screen, the mirror of opening About.
- **Settings menu groups (Oct 5, 06:31):** three groups: Profile, About, Share | Language, Appearance, Maps | Chat settings, Data sources. Between groups 40px from button to button (06:38: half the earlier one-button space, rounded to the 8 scale; within a group they stay 16px apart). The three groups together are centred on the screen height; Sign out stays at the bottom.
- **Station, incidents nearby (Oct 5, 06:44):** the list of fires near the station is titled "Incidents nearby" (group label style, 56px above, 8px to the card), and each incident leads with its map marker (active fire orange circle, resolved dark brown), 24px, 16px before the name. The station's kind (e.g. Volunteers) now sits in the title column too, under the place.
- **Station unit cards (Oct 5, 06:46):** vehicle, crew and aircraft cards keep 16px padding on all sides whatever the spacing preference (data-wf-keepsp), lighter and more compact.
- **Station blades (Oct 5, 06:47):** the crew/command team sheet and the Key figures picker put their title 30px under the blade's top edge, the same as the dialogs (.wf-dlg), fixed whatever the spacing preference; their grabber touch zone is 24px tall so it never covers the header's round button.
